Method: 该研究采用了一种新颖的分析框架,结合随机微积分、几何泛函分析和随机线性代数的工具。核心思想是利用线路对单个门变化的响应特性进行分析,而不依赖于高阶酉设计的收敛性控制。通过分析线路参数的微小扰动如何影响整体酉算子,建立了复杂度的下界估计。

Result: 证明了对于系统大小为n的随机酉量子线路,在所有2≤T≤4^n的时间范围内,其常数误差线路复杂度以Ω(T/log T)的速率增长。该界不依赖于系统大小n(除了时间上限),相比之前基于谱间隙和酉设计的方法,改进了poly(n)的因子。

Conclusion: 本文通过创新性地结合多个数学领域的工具,成功证明了随机酉量子线路的复杂度几乎线性增长,达到了Ω(T/log T)的紧下界。这一结果显著改进了现有界,为理解量子线路的不可压缩性和复杂度演化提供了更精确的理论基础,且该方法避免了对高阶酉设计收敛性的依赖,具有更广的适用性。

Abstract: A random unitary quantum circuit is expected to be incompressible for exponentially long times. We show that the constant-error circuit complexity of a random unitary circuit grows almost linearly with time as $Ω(T/\log T)$. The bound holds for all $2\leq T\leq 4^n$ where $n$ is the system size, and involves no other $n$-dependence. This improves previous lower bounds derived from spectral gaps and unitary designs by a factor of $\mathrm{poly}(n)$. Drawing on insights from stochastic calculus, geometric functional analysis, and randomized linear algebra, our approach exploits the circuit's response to variations of individual gates and requires no control over convergence to high-order unitary designs.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum Compiler Design for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17465)
*Chenghong Zhu,Jiahan Chen,Keming He,Hongshun Yao,Zhaohui Yang,Jin-Guo Liu,Anbang Wu,Xiaotong Ni,Xingsheng Luan,Zhuo Fu,Shenggen Zheng,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向量子纠错保护的容错量子计算编译器的全栈设计,涵盖逻辑层QEC编译、物理层QEC实现和解码器运行时集成三个层次,系统梳理了表面码lattice surgery、qLDPC码、magic-state蒸馏等编译技术,以及超导、离子阱、中性原子等硬件平台的实现方案,并指出跨层优化、编译器-解码器协同设计等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的量子计算依赖于容错量子计算(FTQC),需要量子纠错(QEC)来抑制物理错误以支持可靠的逻辑操作。这要求量子编译器超越通用电路优化,转向编码感知和协议结构化的全栈编译。现有编译器必须处理算法级操作到逻辑门集的降维、编码数据和辅助资源的协调、硬件约束下的逻辑操作与重复校验子提取的实现,以及向实时解码提供测量流等复杂任务,因此需要系统性地综述面向QEC的编译器设计全貌。

Method: 本文采用分层综述方法,将现有工作组织为三个相互作用的层次:(1)逻辑层QEC编译:综述表面码lattice-surgery编译器、超越表面码的code-surgery框架(包括新兴的qLDPC方法),以及非Clifford操作(如magic-state蒸馏和码切换)的编译支持;(2)物理层QEC实现:调研超导、离子阱和中性原子平台上的硬件感知QEC实现;(3)解码器运行时集成:考察解码器模型、实时解码系统,以及在容错执行期间闭合反馈回路的帧管理机制。

Result: 本文系统梳理了容错量子计算编译器的完整技术栈,涵盖从逻辑门集降维、表面码与qLDPC码的编译方案、多种量子硬件平台的物理实现,到实时解码与反馈机制的全流程。文章提供了各层次现有工作的全面总结,识别了跨层优化、qLDPC编译、编译器-解码器协同设计、运行时自适应性,以及集成可基准测试的FTQC编译栈等关键开放挑战,并维护了活跃更新的论文列表资源。

Conclusion: 容错量子计算编译器设计需要跨逻辑、物理和解码器运行时三个层次的协同优化。尽管在表面码编译、硬件映射和实时解码等方面已有显著进展,但在跨层优化、新兴qLDPC码的高效编译、编译器与解码器的协同设计、运行时自适应调度,以及构建统一可基准测试的编译工具链等方面仍存在重要挑战。推进这些方向将是实现大规模容错量子计算的关键,需要从算法、编译、硬件到解码的全栈协同创新。

Abstract: Scalable quantum computation is expected to rely on fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC), in which quantum error correction (QEC) suppresses physical errors sufficiently to support reliable logical operations. This requires quantum compilation to move beyond general-purpose circuit optimization toward encoding-aware and protocol-structured compilation across the full stack of fault-tolerant quantum computers. Beyond circuit synthesis and hardware mapping, an FTQC compiler must lower algorithm-level operations into the logical gate set supported by the chosen code, coordinate encoded data and ancilla resources, realize logical operations together with repeated syndrome extraction under hardware constraints, and provide the resulting measurement stream to real-time decoding.
  This survey presents a full-stack view of compiler design for QEC-protected quantum computation. We organize existing work into three interacting layers: logical-level QEC compilation, physical-level QEC realization, and decoder runtime integration. At the logical level, we review surface-code lattice-surgery compilers, beyond-surface-code code-surgery frameworks including emerging qLDPC approaches, and compilation support for non-Clifford operations such as magic-state distillation and code switching. At the physical level, we survey hardware-aware QEC realization on superconducting, trapped-ion, and neutral-atom platforms. We further examine decoder models, real-time decoding systems, and frame-management mechanisms that close the feedback loop during fault-tolerant execution. Finally, we identify open challenges in cross-layer optimization, qLDPC compilation, compiler-decoder co-design, runtime adaptivity, and the development of integrated and benchmarkable FTQC compilation stacks. An actively maintained paper list is available at: github.com/chenghongz/QEC-compiler-design.

</details>


### [96] [Predictive Structure Behind Rare Outcomes in Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17482)
*Myeongsu Kim,Travis Humble,Sabre Kais*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机量子电路的稀有输出是否揭示了超越选择事件本身的中间物理组织结构。通过比较未条件化的随机正态电路(RN)和峰值选择的随机峰值电路(RP),发现RP电路具有更强的成对关联、概率重分配以及更强的峰值导向干涉,但双分区纠缠和输出扩散减弱。研究证实稀有输出揭示的中间物理组织在新动力学下仍然有用,并可指导电路构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上,随机量子电路的稀有输出被视为终端统计量。本研究旨在探究这些稀有输出是否揭示了在选择事件之后仍然有用的中间组织结构,即稀有输出是否包含超越其终端统计特性的物理意义和实用价值。

Method: 1. 比较分析:对比未条件化的随机正态电路(RN)和峰值选择的随机峰值电路(RP)(来自同一局部生成器),在n=8-16量子比特范围内分析成对关联、概率重分配、双分区纠缠和输出扩散特性;2. 精确全后缀分解:揭示更强的峰值导向干涉;3. 结构持久性测试:在n=8-14、四个标称深度下测试结构分离和新事件优势的持久性,使用独立校准的阈值;4. 结构前缀评分:在不使用终端输出的情况下预测新事件概率;5. 相位扰动实验:通过保持布居数的相位扰乱识别相对相位组织的功能贡献;6. 轨迹引导初始化:测试RP轨迹引导初始化相比Haar初始化在局部优化下对高峰值产率的改进;7. 多变量引导:测试中间多变量引导对构造态的新延续敏感性的改进效果。

Result: 1. RP电路相比RN电路表现出更强的成对关联和概率重分配,但双分区纠缠和输出扩散减弱;2. 精确全后缀分解显示RP具有更强的峰值导向干涉;3. 结构分离和新事件优势在四个标称深度(n=8-14)下持续存在,在固定独立校准阈值下,深度扩展中RP富集度为2.01-7.15倍;4. 结构前缀评分在不使用终端输出的情况下成功预测新事件概率;5. 保持布居数的相位扰乱实验确认了相对相位组织在所有测试设置中的功能贡献,包括主深度留出验证;6. RP轨迹引导初始化在通用局部优化下比Haar初始化产生更高的峰值产率;7. 中间多变量引导比仅峰值引导在相同尺寸-深度网格上更好地改进构造态的新延续敏感性。

Conclusion: 稀有输出揭示了具有物理意义的中间组织结构,这种结构在新动力学条件下仍然保持有用性,并可有效指导量子电路的构建。研究证明稀有量子事件不仅仅是终端统计特性,而是包含了可在未来动力学演化中发挥作用的中间物理信息,为理解和利用量子电路的稀有输出提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Rare outputs of random quantum circuits are usually treated as terminal statistics. We ask whether they reveal intermediate organization that remains useful beyond the selecting future. Comparing unconditioned random-normal (RN) and peak-selected random-peaked (RP) circuits from the same local generator, we find stronger pairwise correlations and probability redistribution but reduced bipartite and output spreading in RP across n=8-16. Exact full-suffix decomposition reveals stronger peak-directed interference. The structural separation and fresh-future advantage persist across four nominal depths at n=8-14, with 2.01-7.15 fold RP enrichment in the depth extensions at fixed, independently calibrated thresholds. Within the unconditioned ensemble, a structural prefix score predicts fresh-event probability without using the evaluated circuits' terminal outcomes. Population-preserving phase scrambling identifies a functional contribution from relative-phase organization across the tested settings, including primary-depth held-out confirmation. RP trajectory-guided initialization improves high-peak yield over Haar initialization under common local refinement. Intermediate multivariate guidance also improves the fresh-continuation susceptibility of constructed states beyond peak-only guidance across the same size-depth grid. Rare outputs thus reveal intermediate physical organization that remains useful under new dynamics and can guide circuit construction.

</details>


### [97] [Query-optimal quantum simulation of Lindblad evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17490)
*Chunhao Wang,Christopher Ye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Lindblad演化模拟问题，提出了一种在块编码模型下具有最优加性依赖的量子算法，实现了查询复杂度的上下界匹配，从而解决了长期存在的查询复杂度gap问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Lindblad演化模拟算法的查询复杂度为乘性上界O(t·polylog(1/ε))，而Hamilton模拟提供的下界是加性的Ω(t + polylog(1/ε))，两者之间存在gap。这个乘性依赖是否必要一直是悬而未决的开放问题，需要确定模拟Lindblad演化在根本上需要多少oracle访问次数。

Method: 采用transducer框架来降低组合一阶近似演化通道的查询成本；使用不同长度的重用电路的线性组合来抑制催化剂移除误差；在块编码(block-encoding)模型下设计算法，实现演化时间和精度的最优加性依赖。

Result: 成功构造了一种算法，在查询复杂度上达到了最优的加性依赖形式Õ(t + polylog(1/ε))，与下界相匹配，从而关闭了查询复杂度的gap。尽管额外的门复杂度高于现有算法，但在查询复杂度方面实现了理论最优。

Conclusion: 本文从根本上解决了Lindblad演化模拟需要多少oracle访问的问题，证明了加性依赖是可达到的，查询复杂度的最优性已经实现。剩余的挑战是如何在保持最优查询复杂度的同时，进一步优化门复杂度以达到整体最优。

Abstract: For the problem of simulating Lindblad evolution for time $t$ to precision $ε$, Hamiltonian simulation provides an additive query lower bound, informally, $Ω(t + \mathrm{polylog}(1/ε))$. However, the best previously known algorithms for general Lindblad simulation achieve a multiplicative upper bound, informally, $\mathcal{O}(t\,\mathrm{polylog}(1/ε))$, in gate complexity. It has remained open whether this multiplicative dependence is necessary. In this paper, we close the gap in query complexity by giving an algorithm with optimal additive dependence on evolution time and precision in the block-encoding model. Our approach uses the transducer framework to reduce the query cost of composing first-order approximations to the evolution channel, together with linear combinations of reuse circuits of different lengths to suppress catalyst-removal error.
  Although our additional gate complexity is higher than that of existing algorithms, our optimal query complexity resolves the question of how much oracle access is fundamentally necessary and identifies the remaining challenge to achieve the optimal gate complexity.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Hardware: Adaptive Algorithmic Control by State-Proxy Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17497)
*Jianlong Lu,Hongrui Zhang,Vishal Sharathchandra Bajpe,Thorsten Koch,Ying Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应算法控制(A2C)范式,通过优化量子计算中有限计算资源的分配方式而非依赖硬件改进,在量子优化问题中实现了22%至超过100,000%的低能态采样概率提升,证明了软件层面的资源优化是推进量子计算的重要补充路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的进步主要依赖于硬件改进,但本文认为通过优化有限计算资源在量子计算过程中的分配方式同样可以获得显著性能提升。传统方法未能充分利用量子态信息来动态调整资源分配,导致计算资源未被最优化使用。因此需要一种新的软件范式来重新组织计算资源,以提升量子计算性能。

Method: 提出了自适应算法控制(A2C)软件范式,其核心是状态-代理均衡定理(State-Proxy Equalization theorem)。该定理证明了对于状态衍生的代理误差泛函,最优资源分配应均衡累积计算难度而非物理时间。方法的关键是直接从演化的量子态推断所需的计算难度,避免了对指数级大的多体能谱的显式重构。通过精确模拟、大规模超算计算和IBM量子硬件实验相结合的方式进行验证。

Result: 在包含多达156个量子比特的量子优化问题中,A2C在相同电路深度和测量预算的条件下,将低能态采样概率提升了22%至超过100,000%。这些结果在精确模拟、超级计算机大规模计算以及IBM量子硬件实验中均得到验证,展示了跨平台的一致性和有效性。

Conclusion: 研究证明量子计算性能不仅取决于硬件能力,还取决于有限计算资源的组织方式。自适应算法控制作为一种软件层面的优化方法,为推进量子计算提供了与硬件改进互补的重要途径。通过智能分配计算资源,可以在不改变硬件的情况下显著提升量子算法的性能,为量子计算的实际应用开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing have been driven primarily by improvements in hardware. Here we show that substantial gains can instead arise from how finite computational resources are allocated throughout a quantum computation. We introduce Adaptive Algorithmic Control (A2C), a software paradigm founded on a State-Proxy Equalization theorem, which proves that the optimal allocation for a state-derived proxy-error functional equalizes cumulative computational hardness rather than physical time. The required computational hardness is inferred directly from the evolving quantum state, avoiding explicit reconstruction of the exponentially large many-body spectrum. Across quantum optimization problems containing up to 156 qubits, combining exact simulations, large-scale supercomputer computations and IBM quantum hardware experiments, A2C improves the low-energy sampling probabilities by $22\%$ to over $100,000\%$ under matched circuit depths and measurement budgets. These results demonstrate that quantum computational performance depends not only on hardware capabilities, but also on how finite computational resources are organized, establishing adaptive algorithmic control as a complementary software pathway for advancing quantum computation.

</details>


### [99] [Antidistinguishability of states in General Probabilistic Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17498)
*Satyaki Manna,Anandamay Das Bhowmik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义概率论(GPTs)框架下研究状态的反可区分性,建立了反可区分性的精细化概念和理论结果,分析了多边形理论中的反可区分性条件,并通过随机排斥码探测了多边形理论的非经典性,发现某些多边形模型可以超越量子最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反可区分性是量子信息理论中的重要概念,但在更广泛的广义概率论框架下缺乏系统研究。需要建立反可区分性的精确数学表述,探索不同物理理论中反可区分性的特征,并理解其与非经典性的关系,特别是在多边形理论等非量子理论中的表现。

Method: 首先在广义概率论框架下形式化定义反可区分性、强反可区分性和等反可区分性,对测量效应施加额外约束。推导这些概念之间的一般关系,并根据状态空间的仿射维数建立等反可区分集合基数的上界。然后专门研究多边形理论中的反可区分性,获得状态集反可区分的充要条件。最后利用随机排斥码作为探针,通过编码状态的反可区分性来检验多边形理论的非经典性能。

Result: 建立了广义概率论中反可区分性的完整理论框架和数学结果。证明了多边形模型中所有纯态集合都是反可区分的,并识别出强反可区分和等反可区分状态的广泛族类。在随机排斥码任务中,发现某些多边形模型的性能可以超越量子理论的最优值,但在大多边形极限下其最优性能收敛到量子极限。

Conclusion: 本研究在广义概率论框架下系统建立了反可区分性理论,揭示了多边形理论中反可区分性的丰富结构。研究表明多边形理论可以展现超越量子理论的非经典行为,但这种优势在特定极限下会消失,这为理解不同物理理论的信息处理能力边界提供了新视角,也为探索后量子理论的资源优势提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate antidistinguishability of states within the framework of general probabilistic theories (GPTs). We formulate antidistinguishability, strong and equal antidistinguishability as refined notions that imposed additional constraint on the measurement effects. We establish general results relating these notions of antidistinguishability and derive an upper bound on the cardinality of equally antidistinguishable sets in terms of the affine dimension of the state space. We then study antidistinguishability in polygonal theories, obtaining conditions for antidistinguishability of a set of states. In consequence, we show that the set of all pure states in a polygon model is antidistinguishable. Additionally, we identify broad families of strongly and equally antidistinguishable states. Finally, using Random Exclusion Codes, whose success probability is governed by the antidistinguishability of different sets of encoding states, we probe the nonclassicality of polygon theories. We find that certain polygon models can outperform the optimal quantum value, while their optimal performance converges to the quantum limit in the large-polygon limit.

</details>


### [100] [A Case Study on Noise Resilient Operator Selection in Adaptive Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17501)
*Soorya Haravu,Mafalda Ramôa,Bharath Sambasivam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了硬件噪声对ADAPT-VQE算法中算符选择步骤的影响，并展示了量子误差缓解技术（动态解耦、零噪声外推和Pauli旋转）的组合应用能够有效恢复算法在噪声环境下的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注硬件噪声对ADAPT-VQE参数优化步骤的影响，但噪声对其关键的算符选择步骤的影响仍未得到充分探索。理解并缓解这一影响对于在近期量子硬件上成功实施ADAPT-VQE算法至关重要。

Method: 以线性H₃分子为测试案例，系统研究了多种噪声通道（相干噪声和非相干噪声）对算符选择步骤的影响。采用三种量子误差缓解技术：动态解耦（dynamical decoupling）、零噪声外推（zero noise extrapolation）和Pauli旋转（Pauli twirling），并探索这些技术的适当组合方案。

Result: 研究表明，尽管选择准则对某些噪声具有天然的鲁棒性，但当噪声率足够高时，相干噪声和非相干噪声都会阻止算法收敛。通过适当组合应用量子误差缓解技术，能够成功恢复ADAPT-VQE算法的收敛特性。

Conclusion: 量子误差缓解技术的组合应用能够显著改善ADAPT-VQE算法在硬件噪声存在下的性能，使算法在噪声环境中实现收敛。这些研究结果为在近期量子硬件上实现ADAPT-VQE算法提供了有价值的见解和实用指导。

Abstract: Hardware noise has been shown to significantly impact the accuracy of ADAPT-VQE, a ground state preparation algorithm. While previous work has studied the impact of noise on its parameter optimization step, its impact on the critical operator selection step remains comparatively unexplored. In this work, we examine the impact of a variety of noise channels on this step, using a linear H$_3$ molecule as a test case. We show that, despite the selection criterion's natural resilience to some noise, both coherent and incoherent noise can prevent convergence for sufficiently high noise rates. We employ quantum error mitigation techniques--dynamical decoupling, zero noise extrapolation, and Pauli twirling--and show that when combined appropriately, these techniques are capable of restoring a successful convergence profile. Our results highlight how error mitigation can improve the performance of ADAPT-VQE and enable convergence in the presence of hardware noise, offering valuable insights into the implementation of the algorithm on near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [101] [Regularized barycentric Rényi divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17517)
*Milán Mosonyi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了重心Rényi散度的可加性问题，证明了对于任何可加且单调的量子相对熵选择，正则化的重心Rényi散度在严格正输入上等同于最小重心Rényi散度，从而确立了最小重心Rényi散度是唯一具有可加性的重心Rényi散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重心Rényi散度作为定义多元量子Rényi散度的替代方法，其可加性问题尚未完全解决。此前仅在所有相对熵均选择Umegaki相对熵时建立了可加性，而这也是唯一存在显式表达的情况（称为最小重心Rényi散度）。对于一般情况下由不同可加单调量子相对熵构造的重心Rényi散度是否具有可加性，以及是否存在其他可加的重心Rényi散度，这些基本理论问题亟需解答。

Method: 本文采用理论证明的方法，研究正则化重心Rényi散度与最小重心Rényi散度之间的关系。核心技术路线是分析由任意可加且单调的量子相对熵集合构造的重心Rényi散度的变分表达式，通过正则化技术处理张量积下的行为，并在严格正输入的条件下建立等价性关系，最终推导出可加性的唯一性结果。

Result: 研究证明了对于任意选择的可加且单调的量子相对熵，正则化的重心Rényi散度在严格正输入上与最小重心Rényi散度重合。这一结果直接推导出：最小重心Rényi散度是唯一具有可加性的重心Rényi散度。该结论完全刻画了重心Rényi散度的可加性结构，确立了Umegaki相对熵构造的特殊地位。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了重心Rényi散度的可加性问题，证明了最小重心Rényi散度（基于Umegaki相对熵）是唯一可加的重心Rényi散度。这一唯一性结果不仅完善了重心Rényi散度的理论框架，也为量子信息论中多元量子散度的研究提供了重要的理论基础，明确了在寻求可加性质时应采用的构造方法。

Abstract: Barycentric Rényi divergences were introduced in [Mosonyi, Bunth, Vrana, Linear Algebra and its Applications, 2024] as an alternative to standard Kubo-Ando constructions to define multivariate quantum Rényi divergences. They are defined via a variational expression and depend on a finite collection of quantum relative entropies $D^{q_x}$. When all the relative entropies are monotone under CPTP maps then so are the corresponding barycentric Rényi divergences, and when all the relative entropies are additive then the corresponding barycentric Rényi divergences are subadditive under tensor product. Additivity has only been established before for the case where all $D^{q_x}$ are chosen to be the Umegaki relative entropy, which is also the only case where the barycentric Rényi divergence (called the minimal one) admits an explicit expression.
  Here we settle the problem of additivity by showing that for any choice of additive and monotone quantum relative entropies, the regularized barycentric Rényi divergence coincides with the minimal barycentric Rényi divergence on strictly positive inputs. This in turn implies that the only additive barycentric Rényi divergence is the minimal one.

</details>

### [102] [A LooKK at the Higuchi Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16103)
*Dieter Lüst,Joaquin Masias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了de Sitter空间中大质量自旋2场必须满足的Higuchi界($m^2\geq 2H^2$)在高维引力紧化到四维de Sitter空间时对Kaluza-Klein引力子塔的约束,提出一致紧化的KK引力子不应违反此界,并在多个具体例子中进行了验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维引力理论紧化到四维de Sitter空间时会产生Kaluza-Klein引力子塔,这些引力子的质量由内部几何决定。由于de Sitter空间中的大质量自旋2场必须满足Higuchi界,这对紧化几何提出了约束。需要检验一致的紧化方案是否自然满足这一界,以及何时可能出现违反,从而理解de Sitter空间紧化的一致性条件

Method: 作者提出一致紧化的KK引力子不会违反Higuchi界的猜想,并在多个具体紧化方案中进行检验:(1)在由Casimir能量稳定的圆周紧化中分析质量谱;(2)研究具有负张力膜的翘曲区间紧化;(3)在群流形紧化中考察内部曲率与Hubble尺度的关系;(4)将可能导致违反的光滑形变提升为模空间参数,通过求解10维运动方程来检验约束的满足情况

Result: 研究发现:(1)圆周紧化中,除非圆周收缩到物种标度以下,否则Higuchi界成立;(2)负张力膜的翘曲区间紧化中,无论区间长度如何,KK引力子塔在$m^2\geq \tfrac{9}{4}H^2$处存在能隙,自动满足更强的约束;(3)在群流形紧化中,当内部曲率不参数化地大于Hubble尺度时,光滑形变似乎可以违反Higuchi界;(4)但将这些形变提升为模参数后,在求解10维运动方程并对模参数极值化时,违反情况消失

Conclusion: 研究支持了一致紧化的KK引力子塔不违反Higuchi界的猜想。在所检验的各种紧化方案中,要么约束自然满足,要么表面上的违反在正确处理动力学模参数后消失。这表明Higuchi界为de Sitter空间的高维引力紧化提供了重要的一致性检验,可能是筛选有效紧化方案的有力工具

Abstract: Massive spin 2 fields on de Sitter must satisfy the Higuchi bound, $m^2\geq 2H^2$. Compactifications of higher dimensional gravity to $\mathrm{dS}_4$ come with a tower of Kaluza-Klein gravitons whose masses are fixed by the internal geometry, so the bound becomes a constraint on the compactification. We propose that the KK gravitons of a consistent compactification never violate it, and test this in a few examples. On a circle stabilized by Casimir energy the bound holds unless the circle shrinks below the species scale, and on a warped interval with negative tension branes the tower is gapped at $m^2\geq \tfrac{9}{4}H^2$ for any length of the interval. On group manifolds, we argue that if the internal curvature is not parametrically larger than the Hubble scale, the bound can apparently be violated by smooth deformations. The violation disappears once one promotes these deformations to moduli, which are immediately extremized when solving the 10d equations of motion.

</details>


### [103] [Canonical differential equations for Feynman integrals from $\mathcal{A}$-hypergeometric systems in the Schwinger representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16107)
*Mateo Jimenez-Santacruz,Cristhiam Lopez-Arcos,Alexander Quintero Velez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于D-模方法和GKZ超几何系统直接构造Feynman积分正则微分方程的新途径,通过Schwinger表示和广义Euler积分形式,结合Newton多面体的facet约化和Frobenius基构造Pfaffian系统,最终得到正则ε-形式,其正则基的维数由GKZ系统的完整秩决定,可提供比IBP方法更精简的微分系统描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Feynman积分微分方程构造方法(如Lee-Pomeransky表示和IBP约化)存在冗余性问题。本文旨在发展一种更系统的D-模理论框架,利用GKZ超几何系统的数学结构直接构造正则微分方程,以获得维数更低、更本质的微分系统描述,避免传统方法中主积分数量可能过多的问题。

Method: 1) 从Schwinger参数表示出发,识别相关的广义Euler积分及其GKZ超几何系统,作为Lee-Pomeransky表示的替代方案;2) 利用Newton多面体的facet结构直接识别约化关系,减少微分系统中的变量数;3) 使用Frobenius基构造相应的Pfaffian系统,同时获得奇点轨迹的直接信息;4) 通过适当的有理化变量变换,将系统转化为正则ε-形式;5) 正则基的维数由GKZ系统的完整秩(holonomic rank)确定。

Result: 在所考察的实例中,基于GKZ系统构造的正则基维数小于或等于通过分部积分(IBP)方法得到的主积分数量,实现了对微分系统的精简描述。该方法成功地将Feynman积分的微分方程构造问题与GKZ超几何系统的D-模理论联系起来,提供了一种更为系统和优化的构造途径。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从GKZ超几何系统到Feynman积分正则微分方程的系统构造框架,通过D-模方法、Newton多面体约化和Frobenius基技术,实现了比传统IBP方法更精简的微分系统描述。该方法的核心优势在于利用GKZ系统的完整秩自然确定正则基维数,为Feynman积分的解析计算提供了更高效的数学工具,展示了代数几何方法在量子场论计算中的深刻应用价值。

Abstract: We follow a $D$-module approach to the construction of canonical differential equations for Feynman integrals directly from their Gel'fand-Kapranov-Zelevinsky (GKZ) hypergeometric systems. Starting from the Schwinger representation, we identify the associated generalized Euler integral and its GKZ system, as an alternative to the Lee-Pomeransky representation. The generalized Euler formulation allows reductions associated with facets of the Newton polytope to be identified directly, reducing the number of variables in the resulting differential systems. We construct the associated Pfaffian systems using Frobenius bases, which also provide direct access to the singular loci. After a suitable rationalizing change of variables, the systems are brought into canonical $ε$-form. A key feature of the construction is that the dimension of the canonical basis is determined by the holonomic rank of the GKZ system and, for the examples considered, it is smaller than or equal to the number of master integrals obtained from integration-by-parts, thus providing a reduced description of the differential system.

</details>


### [104] [Boundary duals of bulk detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16109)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Robert C. Myers,Kelly Wurtz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了反德西特空间(AdS)中Unruh-DeWitt探测器的边界对偶问题,利用HKLL重构方案将体探测器映射为边界上的弥散探测器,并分析了AdS₃庞加莱贴片中的纠缠收集现象,同时解决了体中类空分离探测器的微观因果性与边界HKLL探测器可能直接重叠之间的表面张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS/CFT对偶框架下,理解体中局域探测器(Unruh-DeWitt探测器)如何映射到边界理论是一个基本问题。需要建立体探测器与边界探测器之间的明确对应关系,并利用这一对应关系研究量子信息任务(如纠缠收集)在全息对偶中的表现,同时需要解决体中微观因果性与边界理论中探测器重叠之间的潜在矛盾。

Method: 采用HKLL(Hamilton-Kabat-Lifschytz-Lowe)重构方案,将体中的局域探测器关联到边界上的弥散探测器,其中弥散函数的选择依赖于HKLL核的选择。在AdS₃的庞加莱贴片中,针对体Unruh-DeWitt探测器与边界探测器配对、以及两个体探测器配对两种情况,计算纠缠收集量。通过将耦合常数λ展开到二阶,评估双探测器密度矩阵来量化纠缠收集效应。分析类空分离体探测器的微观因果性与对偶HKLL边界探测器可能直接重叠之间的表面矛盾。

Result: 成功建立了体Unruh-DeWitt探测器与边界弥散探测器之间的对偶关系,具体形式由HKLL核决定。在AdS₃庞加莱贴片中计算了两种配置(体-边界探测器对和体-体探测器对)的纠缠收集量,得到了耦合常数λ二阶近似下的双探测器密度矩阵。识别并解决了体中类空分离探测器满足微观因果性与边界理论中对应的HKLL探测器可能发生直接重叠之间的表面张力问题。

Conclusion: HKLL重构方案为理解AdS空间中Unruh-DeWitt探测器的边界对偶提供了自然的框架,体中局域探测器对应于边界上的弥散探测器。通过该框架成功分析了AdS₃中的纠缠收集现象,并澄清了体中微观因果性与边界探测器重叠之间并不存在真正的矛盾。这项工作深化了对全息对偶中量子信息处理和因果结构的理解,为进一步研究AdS/CFT框架下的量子信息任务奠定了基础。

Abstract: What is the boundary dual of an Unruh-DeWitt detector in anti-de Sitter space? We argue that this question is naturally answered by the HKLL reconstruction program. A local detector in the bulk can be associated with a smeared detector in the boundary, where the choice of smearing depends on the choice of HKLL kernel. We use this formalism to analyze entanglement harvesting in the Poincaré patch of AdS$_{3}$ for a bulk Unruh-DeWitt detector paired either with a boundary detector or with a second bulk detector. We compute the amount of harvesting in the standard way, namely by evaluating the two-detector density matrix to quadratic order in the coupling $λ$. Additionally, we address an apparent tension between microcausality for spacelike-separated bulk detectors and the fact that the dual HKLL detectors may directly overlap in the boundary theory.

</details>


### [105] [Classification of c=1 CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16111)
*Lorenz Eberhardt,Nat Levine*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了中心荷c=1且具有离散谱的幺正二维共形场论，证明仅存在五种理论：紧致自由玻色子、其Z₂-轨道化理论以及三个由SU(2)₁ WZW模型轨道化得到的例外理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维共形场论中关于c=1离散谱理论的分类一直是理论物理的经典问题，虽然物理学界有相关的"民间传说"认为只存在特定几种理论，但缺乏严格的数学证明。本文旨在为这一分类提供完整的解析证明，并探究态密度与完整CFT结构之间的关系。

Method: 将c=1的模自举问题重新表述为态密度的晶体测度问题，并通过解析方法求解该问题。利用模不变性约束和态密度的数学性质，系统地排除其他可能性，最终确定所有允许的理论。进一步证明了从态密度可以唯一重构完整的共形场论。

Result: 严格证明了c=1离散谱的幺正二维CFT仅有五种：(1)紧致自由玻色子理论；(2)紧致自由玻色子的Z₂-轨道化理论；(3-5)从SU(2)₁ WZW模型通过轨道化构造的三个例外理论。此外，证明了态密度完全确定后，整个CFT结构可以被唯一重构。

Conclusion: 本文为c=1离散谱二维共形场论提供了完整且严格的分类，验证了物理学界长期以来的猜想。通过将模自举问题转化为晶体测度问题并解析求解，不仅给出了所有可能理论的完整列表，还揭示了态密度在确定CFT结构中的核心作用，表明态密度包含了重构整个理论的全部信息。这一结果为理解低中心荷共形场论的结构提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: We completely classify unitary 2d CFTs with central charge $c=1$ and discrete spectrum. We confirm the folklore that the only such theories are the compact free boson, its $\mathbb{Z}_2$-orbifold, and three exceptional theories obtained as orbifolds of the SU(2)$_1$ WZW model. The proof is by recasting the $c=1$ modular bootstrap as a crystalline measure problem for the density of states, and analytically solving it. We also show that, once the density of states is specified, the full CFT can be uniquely reconstructed.

</details>


### [106] [When the gravity path integral describes a statistical average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16113)
*Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Luca V. Iliesiu,Henry W. Lin,Cynthia Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究引力路径积分(GPI)的解释条件,区分了希尔伯特空间内积与边界可观测量统计平均两种解释,并提出更强的正定性条件来约束虫洞振幅,从而限制哪些引力有效场论可嵌入自洽的量子引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力路径积分的物理解释存在歧义:它可能对应希尔伯特空间的内积结构,也可能对应边界可观测量的统计平均。需要明确区分这两种解释的数学条件,并理解它们对虫洞振幅和量子引力理论一致性的约束作用,特别是需要判断GPI计算的是真实统计平均还是仅仅是伪平均。

Method: 1) 推导引力路径积分满足希尔伯特空间内积解释(条件i)的充要条件;2) 推导满足边界可观测量统计平均解释(条件ii)的充要条件,证明条件ii比条件i更强;3) 构造满足条件i但违反条件ii的引力玩具模型作为反例;4) 基于条件ii开展统计系综的自举研究(bootstrap study),分析其对系综矩的约束;5) 分析两类正定性条件对虫洞振幅的限制及其对引力有效场论嵌入量子引力的影响。

Result: 1) 明确了条件i(内积解释)是条件ii(统计平均解释)的必要非充分条件;2) 提出了判断GPI计算真实平均还是伪平均的具体诊断方法;3) 成功构造了满足较弱条件但违反较强条件的引力玩具模型;4) 基于条件ii的自举约束显著强化了相比仅施加条件i所得到的系综矩的界限;5) 两类正定性条件都对虫洞振幅施加了非平凡约束。

Conclusion: 引力路径积分的统计平均解释(条件ii)比希尔伯特空间内积解释(条件i)需要更强的约束条件。更强的正定性条件(ii)为区分真实统计平均与伪平均提供了具体判据,并对虫洞振幅施加了实质性限制,从而约束了哪些引力有效场论可以嵌入自洽的量子引力理论。这为量子引力理论的一致性检验提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: We find the necessary and sufficient conditions for the gravitational path integral (GPI) to admit an interpretation in terms of (i) an inner product in the Hilbert space of open/closed universes or (ii) a statistical average of boundary observables. The conditions needed for (i) are necessary but not sufficient for (ii). Requiring the stronger condition (ii) places additional constraints on all wormhole amplitudes, giving a concrete diagnostic of whether the GPI computes an average or merely a pseudo-average. To exemplify the difference between the two constraints, we present a class of gravitational toy models that satisfy (i) but fail the stronger condition (ii); to emphasize the power of the stronger condition, we initiate a bootstrap study of statistical ensembles based on condition (ii), finding that the resulting constraints substantially sharpen the bounds on the moments of the ensemble obtained by imposing (i). More broadly, both of these positivity conditions impose nontrivial constraints on wormhole amplitudes and thereby restrict which gravitational effective field theories can be embedded in consistent theories of quantum gravity.

</details>


### [107] [Group-averaged baby universe field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16116)
*Hong Zhe Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了婴儿宇宙场论的框架，其中量子是完整的宇宙。通过群平均振幅施加引力约束并定义物理内积，从一维世界线引力开始，证明了婴儿宇宙希尔伯特空间是对称Fock空间，宇宙场满足可交换的Wheeler-DeWitt线性场方程，并探索了更一般拓扑结构下的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空拓扑上的引力求和暗示存在一个婴儿宇宙场论，其量子对应整个宇宙。为了理解这种非常规量子场论的性质，需要从单宇宙理论的群平均振幅出发，通过施加引力约束来构建物理内积和场论描述，并明确其与普通量子场论的本质区别。

Method: 从一维世界线引力入手（可由高维圆柱约化得到），研究配对边界分量的多条非相互作用世界线系统。通过群平均构造物理希尔伯特空间，将婴儿宇宙希尔伯特空间表示为对称Fock空间。利用壳上模式展开宇宙场，每个模式系数是产生和湮灭算符之和，从而明确展示场的可交换性和Wheeler-DeWitt线性场方程。通过对角化模系数恢复高斯路径积分的统计描述。进一步探索圆盘型拓扑（相干移动无边界态，产生Hartle-Hawking波函数）和更一般拓扑（费曼图表示相互作用）。在拓扑Marolf-Maxfield模型中，将圆盘-圆柱截断推广为包含所有拓扑的精确Fock空间表示。

Result: 对于多条非相互作用世界线，婴儿宇宙希尔伯特空间是单宇宙物理希尔伯特空间的对称Fock空间。宇宙场的模系数可交换，满足Wheeler-DeWitt线性场方程。对角化模系数可恢复由高斯路径积分描述的统计解释，壳上场配置标记超选择（α）部门。圆盘型拓扑相干移动无边界态，将Hartle-Hawking波函数作为单点函数激活。对于非平凡拓扑，边界附近的群平均仍然施加相同的线性场方程，相互作用通过α部门上的非高斯测度进入。在Marolf-Maxfield模型中，实现了包含所有拓扑的精确Fock空间表示，并通过相互作用插入关联截断和精确内积。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了婴儿宇宙场论的数学框架，明确展示了其与普通量子场论的关键差异：宇宙场满足可交换的线性Wheeler-DeWitt方程，物理希尔伯特空间具有Fock空间结构，拓扑求和对应场论中的相互作用但保持线性场方程。群平均在施加引力约束和定义物理内积中起核心作用。该框架为理解量子引力中的时空拓扑求和和婴儿宇宙提供了新的场论视角，并在简化模型中实现了包含所有拓扑的精确表示。

Abstract: Gravitational sums over spacetime topology suggest a baby-universe field theory whose quanta are entire universes. We develop such a description from group-averaged amplitudes that impose gravitational constraints and define physical inner products in the single-universe theory. To sharpen the contrast with ordinary QFT, we start with gravity on one-dimensional worldlines, which can arise from reducing higher-dimensional cylinders. For multiple noninteracting worldlines that pair boundary components, the baby-universe Hilbert space is the symmetric Fock space over the physical one-universe Hilbert space, and the universe field expands in the on-shell modes spanning the latter. Each mode multiplies the sum of creation and annihilation operators, so the mode coefficients commute. The mode expansion thus makes explicit the field's commutativity and its Wheeler-DeWitt linear field equation. Diagonalizing the coefficients recovers a statistical description by a Gaussian path integral over on-shell field configurations labelling superselection ($α$-)sectors.
  We take exploratory steps toward more general topologies. Disk-like topologies coherently shift the no-boundary state, turning on the Hartle-Hawking wavefunction as a one-point function. Even with nontrivial topologies represented by interacting Feynman diagrams, we argue that group averaging near each boundary still enforces the same linear field equation, while interactions instead enter through a non-Gaussian measure over $α$-sectors. In the topological Marolf-Maxfield model, we promote a disk-and-cylinder truncation to an exact Fock-space representation of the full theory incorporating all topologies. We also relate the truncated and exact inner products by an insertion of interactions, whose perturbative expansion produces a sum over time evolutions generated by pairs of pants, evoking an analogy to group averaging.

</details>


### [108] [An attempt to derive the LVS from a 10d EFT perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16117)
*Arthur Hebecker,Andreas Schachner,Gaetano Maria Sifo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在通量紧化中稳定模的非微扰标量势，重点研究大体积情形(LVS) AdS势的10维直接推导。文章发现从10维有效场论得到的体积抑制因子与4维超引力结果存在不匹配，这一困惑目前尚未得到令人满意的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者试图从10维有效场论(EFT)直接推导大体积情形(LVS)的AdS标量势，以理解其微观起源。然而，在推导过程中发现体积抑制因子与已知的4维超引力结果存在系统性偏差：10维EFT预期给出更强的体积抑制。这一不匹配需要得到解释，因为它关系到LVS方案的自洽性和有效性。

Method: 研究采用10维有效场论框架分析LVS标量势的各个贡献项。具体分析了：(1)瞬子-反瞬子效应项；(2)单瞬子耦合3-形式通量项；(3)通量稀释因子和Weyl重标度对体积抑制的贡献。同时讨论了胶子凝聚情形，并探讨了Kähler势的对数修正作为可能的解释途径。研究详细梳理了10维到4维约化过程中的逻辑链条。

Result: 从10维EFT计算得到的体积抑制强于4维超引力预期。对于瞬子-反瞬子项和单瞬子-通量耦合项，10维分析给出：通量稀释贡献$1/\sqrt{\mathcal V}$因子（第二项），Weyl重标度到4维爱因斯坦框架贡献$1/{\mathcal V}^2$因子（两项都有）。这导致总体积抑制比4维结果$\exp(-2aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}$、$W_0\exp(-aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}^2$和$W_0^2/{\mathcal V}^3$更强。作者未能找到令人满意的解释来消除这一不匹配。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LVS标量势10维推导中存在的一个重要困惑：体积抑制因子的不匹配问题。虽然Kähler势的对数修正可能是解决方案，但这些修正需要强到在${\mathcal O}(1)$量级上影响LVS方案的结果，而且所需修正的起源仍不清楚。这一问题的存在表明，对于通量紧化中模稳定机制的微观理解仍有重要的缺口需要填补，可能需要超越当前近似框架的新物理洞察。

Abstract: Non-perturbative scalar potentials are a central ingredient for stabilising moduli in flux compactifications. A key target in this context is the direct 10d derivation of the LVS AdS potential, i.e., the scalar potential of the Large Volume Scenario before the uplift. The familiar result, relying on 4d supergravity machinery and large volume expansion, consists of three terms of parametric form $\exp(-2aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}$, $W_0\exp(-aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}^2$, and $W_0^2/{\mathcal V}^3$. In plain 10d EFT language, the first term is an instanton-anti-instanton effect while the second term comes from a single instanton coupled to 3-form flux. When attempting to derive the suppression by the volume ${\mathcal V}$ directly from the 10d EFT, one encounters a puzzle: One expects a flux-dilution factor $1/\sqrt{\mathcal V}$ for the second term and a factor $1/{\mathcal V}^2$ from Weyl rescaling to the 4d Einstein frame for both the first and second term. Thus, the total volume suppression for these two terms is stronger than in the aforementioned 4d EFT expectations. We discuss the underlying logic in detail, also in the gaugino condensation case, but fail to find a satisfactory explanation for the mismatch. While log-corrections to the Kähler potential may be responsible, these would have to be strong enough to affect the outcome of the LVS scheme at the ${\mathcal O}(1)$ level. What is worse, the origin of the required correction remains unclear.

</details>


### [109] [Single-Instance Observables in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16130)
*Brian Swingle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图解展开方法，用于计算固定实现的N-费米子Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK)模型中低权重Majorana弦的热期望值，该方法在固定βJ时按N^(-1/2)的幂次组织展开，每阶可用经典资源多项式地高效计算，并成功应用于四体SYK模型和Maldacena-Qi模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型在量子引力和量子混沌研究中具有重要意义，但其热力学性质和可观测量的精确计算面临挑战。现有方法在处理固定实现的SYK模型时效率有限，特别是在计算Majorana弦的热期望值以及任意虚时间和短实时间关联函数时缺乏系统性的有效方法。此外，对于Maldacena-Qi模型中热场双态与基态的保真度问题也需要更精确的分析工具。

Method: 提出了一种图解展开方法来计算固定实现N-费米子SYK模型中低权重Majorana弦的热期望值。在每个展开阶次，将可观测量表示为耦合常数的多项式有限和，乘以由大N极限下的melon传播子构建的温度依赖核函数。该展开在固定βJ下按N^(-1/2)的幂次组织，每阶可用多项式复杂度的经典计算资源高效评估。该方法扩展到任意虚时间和足够短实时间的关联函数计算。对于四体SYK模型，通过与N≤24的精确对角化结果对比验证方法有效性。进一步将该方法应用于由两个相同SYK实现副本形成的Maldacena-Qi (MQ)模型，识别出与原始SYK哈密顿量相近的类SYK哈密顿量。

Result: 对于四体SYK模型，图解展开方法的预测结果与N≤24规模的精确对角化结果高度吻合，验证了方法的准确性。在Maldacena-Qi模型的应用中，成功识别出一个邻近的类SYK哈密顿量，其热场双态与Maldacena-Qi基态的保真度高于原始SYK哈密顿量的热场双态。该方法在计算效率上表现出多项式复杂度，使得对较大系统尺寸的计算成为可能。展开方法在处理任意虚时间和短实时间关联函数时也显示出良好的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了一种系统性的图解展开框架，用于高效计算固定实现SYK模型中Majorana弦的热期望值和关联函数。该方法以N^(-1/2)为展开参数，具有多项式计算复杂度，在与精确对角化结果的对比中展现出优异的精度。该框架不仅适用于标准SYK模型，还成功扩展到Maldacena-Qi模型，为研究热场双态与基态的关系提供了新的分析工具。这一方法为探索SYK类模型的热力学性质和量子信息特性开辟了新途径，对理解量子引力全息对偶和量子混沌现象具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present a diagrammatic expansion for thermal expectation values of low-weight Majorana strings in a fixed realization of the $N$-fermion Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model. At each order in the expansion, the observable is expressed as a finite sum of polynomials in the couplings multiplied by temperature-dependent kernels built from the large-$N$ melonic propagator. At fixed $βJ$, the expansion is organized in powers of $N^{-1/2}$, and each order can be efficiently evaluated with classical resources polynomial in $N$. The expansion also extends to correlations at arbitrary imaginary times and sufficiently short real times. For the four-body SYK model, we show that the diagrammatic predictions give an excellent account of exact diagonalization results at sizes up to $N=24$. We also apply the method to the Maldacena--Qi (MQ) model formed from two identical copies of a fixed SYK realization. This enables the identification of a nearby SYK-like Hamiltonian whose thermofield double state has higher fidelity with the Maldacena--Qi ground state than the thermofield double of the original SYK Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [110] [Exact group invariant scar towers in two dimensional gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16132)
*João Barata,Kiryl Pakrouski,Andrey V. Sadofyev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在二维规范理论中构造了精确的多体疤痕态塔,通过代数方法实现了低纠缠、长程配对关联的受保护子空间,并揭示了这些疤痕态在晶格场论中的普适性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕态是违反热化的特殊本征态,在规范场论中构造具有非平凡连续极限解释的精确疤痕态塔具有重要理论意义。现有研究缺乏在具有费米子物质场的规范理论中对疤痕态的系统代数构造和物理解释。

Method: 1) 在含有两味无质量基本费米子的二维规范理论中,利用规范中性的η配对算符代数生成疤痕子空间;2) 采用精确对角化方法分析疤痕态的纠缠熵和长程配对关联;3) 通过电荷共轭变换将η塔映射到等电荷模型的矢量味极化扇区;4) 研究有限费米子质量对精确塔结构的破坏机制;5) 在大类晶格场论中验证该疤痕塔的代数实现。

Result: 成功构造了反向带电费米子系统中的精确疤痕态塔,该塔由规范中性η配对算符生成。精确对角化结果显示疤痕态具有反常低的纠缠熵和长程配对关联特性。电荷共轭映射揭示了疤痕态可解释为具有Josephson型相位动力学的相干味模式。有限质量项会破坏精确塔的保护性。该疤痕塔在大量晶格场论家族中可被代数实现,展现了普适性。

Conclusion: 本研究在具有非平凡连续极限的规范理论中实现了不变疤痕子空间的首次系统代数构造,揭示了η配对机制在二维规范理论中产生受保护量子态的普适性。这些疤痕态的低纠缠特性、长程关联以及Josephson型动力学解释为理解规范理论中的弱热化现象和量子相干性提供了新的理论框架,对量子模拟和拓扑量子计算具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We construct exact many-body scar towers in two dimensional gauge theories with two flavors of massless fundamental fermions. For oppositely charged fermions, the scar subspace is generated by a gauge-neutral $η$-pairing operator, and admits a purely algebraic construction. Exact diagonalization methods reveal anomalously low entanglement and long-range pair correlations in the scar states. Charge conjugation maps the $η$-tower to a vector-flavor polarization sector of the equal charge model, yielding an interpretation as a coherent flavor mode with Josephson-like phase dynamics. Finite fermion masses mix the protected pair with an orthogonal channel and destroy the exact tower. The identified scar tower can be algebraically realized in a large family of lattice field theories, revealing a universal character of these states. Our results provide an analytical construction of invariant scar subspaces in a gauge theory with a nontrivial continuum interpretation.

</details>


### [111] [$D$-term and $F$-term potential of non-Abelian D7-branes in IIB Calabi-Yau orientifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16136)
*Daniel Junghans*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过维数约化D7-膜的非阿贝尔DBI作用量,从第一性原理系统推导了IIB型弦理论Calabi-Yau定向折叠紧致化中D7-膜堆产生的4维D项和F项势,考虑了世界体积通量、体通量及场的非阿贝尔性质,为模量稳定化提供了统一的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管D项和F项势的某些方面已通过各种论证被推断出来,但缺乏统一的第一性原理推导。完整势函数是IIB弦理论中模量稳定化(如使用T-膜上提的de Sitter情景)的重要成分,需要系统性地从微观理论出发进行严格推导,同时需要理解KK模积分修正对理论有效性的约束。

Method: 采用维数约化方法,从D7-膜的非阿贝尔Dirac-Born-Infeld(DBI)作用量出发进行系统推导。计算中考虑了世界体积通量、体内ISD通量以及膜场的非阿贝尔特性。详细讨论了Kaluza-Klein(KK)模积分带来的修正效应,并分析这些修正对理论控制参数的约束条件。

Result: 成功推导出完整的4维有效势,该结果推广了先前的表达式,并在适用范围内与已有结果一致,证实了不同方法之间的相互一致性。证明了要求对KK模修正保持控制意味着对Calabi-Yau体积和膜场存在约束边界。此外,还揭示了体通量如何修正8维世界体积理论中的势和BPS方程,该理论支配着非阿贝尔膜的微观动力学。

Conclusion: 本文提供了D7-膜堆产生的4维D项和F项势的统一第一性原理推导,填补了理论空白并验证了不同方法的一致性。KK模修正的控制条件对Calabi-Yau体积和膜场施加了物理约束。研究不仅完善了4维有效作用量理论,还深化了对8维世界体积理论中体通量效应的理解,为IIB弦理论中的模量稳定化和de Sitter构造提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We derive the 4d $D$-term and $F$-term potential generated by a D7-brane stack in Calabi-Yau orientifold compactifications of type IIB string theory, taking into account the effect of worldvolume and ISD bulk fluxes and the non-Abelian nature of the brane fields. This potential is an important ingredient for moduli stabilization in IIB, e.g., in de Sitter scenarios using a T-brane uplift. While aspects of the $D$-term and $F$-term parts of the potential have been inferred before with various arguments, we provide here a unified first-principles derivation of the full potential by dimensionally reducing the non-Abelian DBI action of the D7-branes. Our result generalizes previous expressions and agrees with them where applicable, thus confirming the mutual consistency of the various approaches. Moreover, we provide a thorough discussion of corrections from integrating out KK modes and argue that demanding control over such corrections implies bounds on the Calabi-Yau volume and the brane fields. Aside from these various results for the 4d effective action, our calculation also shows how bulk fluxes correct the potential and the BPS equations in the 8d worldvolume theory that governs the microscopic dynamics of non-Abelian branes.

Result: 成功构造了12维体父理论，其边界条件精确再现了11维超引力C-场的局部通量方程，包括引力I_8修正项；证明了电磁配对和非线性Maxwell-Chern-Simons动力学可从单一12维拓扑模型下降得到；建立了体方程与S^4的Sullivan模型的对应关系；提出了体场的扭曲4-上同伦量子化和体-边界场空间的同伦拉回表述。

Conclusion: 本文为M理论的对称性下降提供了局部动力学机制和场的候选全局表征。通过12维拓扑模型统一导出了11维超引力C-场的关键性质，包括电磁配对、非线性动力学和引力修正。基于假设H和上同伦理论的框架为理解M理论场的量子化和拓扑性质提供了新视角，将高维拓扑理论与物理场论通过体-边界对应系统地联系起来。

Abstract: We initiate a symmetry descent procedure for M-theory engineered quantum field theories. Starting from a higher form BF theory supplemented by a cubic bulk topological interaction, we construct a gauge-invariant bulk-boundary system whose edge modes acquire generalized Maxwell--Chern--Simons dynamics after the introduction of a metric-dependent boundary action. The nonlinear contribution to the boundary equation is induced entirely by the cubic bulk interaction. In the case of twelve dimensional bulk parent, the resulting boundary conditions reproduce the local flux equations of the eleven-dimensional supergravity $C$-field, including the gravitational $I_8$ correction. Thus, the electric--magnetic pairing and the nonlinear Maxwell--Chern--Simons dynamics descend from a single 12D topological model. Using Hypothesis H, we identify the bulk equations with the Sullivan model of $S^4$ and interpret their nonlinear gauge transformations as homotopies. We then propose a twisted 4-cohomotopy quantization of the bulk fields and a homotopy pullback description of the coupled bulk--boundary field space. The construction provides both a local dynamical mechanism for M-theory symmetry descent and a candidate global characterization of its fields.

</details>


### [113] [Separation of variables for non-diagonalisable models: XXX with twisted boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16146)
*Juan Miguel Nieto García*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有缺陷扭曲矩阵的扭曲周期边界条件下的Heisenberg XXX自旋链，利用量子分离变量方法，建立了广义本征向量的"约当TQ方程"刻画，并揭示了真本征向量与高秩广义本征向量在分离基下波函数结构的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子可积系统研究主要针对具有理想边界条件的系统，但当引入缺陷扭曲矩阵时，系统的本征谱结构会发生根本改变，出现广义本征向量而非完备的真本征向量集。如何刻画这类非对角化系统的广义本征向量及其波函数结构是量子可积系统理论中的重要问题，需要发展超越经典TQ方程的新数学框架。

Method: 采用量子分离变量(quantum separation of variables)方法作为核心技术工具。针对具有缺陷扭曲矩阵的扭曲周期边界条件下的Heisenberg XXX自旋链，推导出修正形式的TQ方程——"约当TQ方程"(Jordanian TQ equation)来刻画系统的广义本征向量。通过分析分离基下波函数的结构，区分真本征向量与不同秩的广义本征向量的数学表示形式。

Result: 建立了约当TQ方程作为刻画缺陷扭曲系统广义本征向量的新工具。证明了在分离基下，真本征向量的波函数保持可分离性，而高秩广义本征向量的波函数则表现为弱组合(weak compositions)上的求和形式，揭示了两者在数学结构上的本质差异。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入约当TQ方程，成功解决了具有缺陷扭曲边界的Heisenberg XXX自旋链的广义本征问题。研究表明缺陷扭曲矩阵导致的非对角化特性在量子分离变量框架下有清晰的数学表征：真本征向量与广义本征向量在波函数结构上存在根本区别。该结果为研究更广泛的非对角化量子可积系统提供了理论基础和技术路线。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg XXX spin chain with twisted periodic boundary conditions with a defective twist matrix. Using quantum separation of variables, we show that the generalised eigenvectors of this system can be characterised in terms of a modified TQ equation that we have named "Jordanian TQ equation". We also show that the wavefunction in the separated basis is only separable for true eigenvectors, while the wavefunction for generalised eigenvectors of higher rank is given instead by a sum over weak compositions.

</details>


### [114] [The Role of Completeness in Probing Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16153)
*Yuya Kusuki,Hiroyasu Tajima,Shion Yamashika*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称性破缺的完备性测度问题,提出对数特征函数(LCF)相比纠缠不对称性(EA)在描述对称性恢复动力学方面具有显著优势,特别是在热力学极限下能区分不同初态的弛豫动力学并识别离散对称性Mpemba效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠不对称性(EA)作为对称性破缺的常用测度存在局限性:在热力学极限下会饱和到相同值,导致无法区分不同初态的弛豫曲线,也无法识别离散对称性的Mpemba效应。不完备的对称性破缺测度可能遗漏对称性恢复动力学的关键特征,因此需要研究完备的测度体系来更全面地刻画多体系统中的对称性破缺动力学。

Method: 1. 研究对数特征函数(LCF,也称为弦序参数)的完备性质,该函数族对于有限群的对称保持操作下的精确独立同分布纯态转换是完备的。2. 将LCF推广到混合态,引入基于保真度的扩展版本。3. 在自旋链淬火和单量子比特退极化噪声系统中进行数值演示。4. 为量子场论中基于保真度的LCF发展副本构造方法,并推导共形场论的解析结果。

Result: 相比纠缠不对称性(EA),对数特征函数(LCF)展现出两个关键优势:第一,对于有限对称群G,EA在热力学极限下受log|G|限制并趋于饱和,而LCF可以保持广延性,其系数依赖于初态和时间演化,因此能够持续区分不同初态的弛豫动力学并识别离散对称性Mpemba效应。第二,LCF的不同分量可以表现出不同的弛豫和交叉行为,即使在EA不显示交叉的情况下也能捕捉到动力学差异。在自旋链淬火实验和单量子比特系统中验证了这些优势,并在共形场论框架下得到了解析结果。

Conclusion: 完备性不仅是量子信息中单调族的重要数学性质,在多体物理系统中也具有关键的物理意义。对数特征函数作为完备的对称性破缺测度,能够克服纠缠不对称性在热力学极限下的饱和问题,有效区分不同初态的对称性恢复动力学,并识别离散对称性Mpemba效应。LCF的多分量特性提供了更丰富的动力学信息,为研究量子多体系统中的对称性破缺和恢复过程提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: Completeness is widely recognized in quantum information as an important property of a family of monotones, because it ensures that no information relevant to state conversion is lost. We show that completeness is also physically important in many-body systems: an incomplete measure of symmetry breaking can miss essential features of symmetry-restoration dynamics. We study the logarithmic characteristic function (LCF), also known as the string order parameter, whose full family is complete for exact i.i.d. pure-state conversion under symmetry-preserving operations for finite groups. We introduce a fidelity-based extension of the LCF to mixed states and identify two concrete advantages over entanglement asymmetry (EA). First, for a finite symmetry group $G$, EA is bounded by $\log |G|$ and can approach the same saturated value for different initial states in the thermodynamic limit, making their relaxation curves indistinguishable and preventing the identification of a discrete-symmetry Mpemba effect. By contrast, the LCF can remain extensive, with a coefficient that depends on the initial state and time, and therefore continues to distinguish their relaxation dynamics. Second, different LCF components can exhibit distinct relaxation and crossing behavior, including cases in which EA shows no crossing. We demonstrate these advantages in spin-chain quenches and a single-qubit system under depolarizing noise. We also develop a replica construction for the fidelity-based LCF in quantum field theory and derive analytical results for conformal field theory.

</details>


### [115] [Making the Great Desert Bloom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16154)
*Jacques Distler,Andreas Karch*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了"大沙漠"假设(弱尺度与普朗克尺度之间无新物理)的深刻意义：它预言标准模型具有半经典的AdS₄真空及其CFT₃对偶理论,并在合理假设下必须伴随至少一个显著大于普朗克长度的额外维度。文章还提出了标准模型可能的CFT₃对偶候选理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理学中"大沙漠"假设通常被视为噩梦场景,即弱尺度和普朗克尺度之间(除中微子质量外)不存在新物理。本文旨在挑战这一悲观观点,论证大沙漠假设实际上具有重大且非平凡的理论预言能力,能够对标准模型的真空结构和额外维度的存在做出明确预测。

Method: 本文采用理论物理分析方法,结合引力/规范对偶(AdS/CFT对应)的框架来研究大沙漠假设的理论后果。通过分析标准模型在极高能标下的行为,利用反德西特空间(AdS₄)与共形场论(CFT₃)的对偶关系,推导出大沙漠假设对真空结构和额外维度的约束条件,并构造可能的CFT₃对偶理论候选。

Result: 研究得出以下关键结果:(1)大沙漠假设预言标准模型存在半经典的四维反德西特空间(AdS₄)真空;(2)该真空具有三维共形场论(CFT₃)对偶描述;(3)在合理的理论假设下,必然存在至少一个尺度显著大于普朗克长度的额外空间维度;(4)文章识别并提出了一个具体的CFT₃理论作为标准模型对偶的可信候选。

Conclusion: 本文颠覆了对"大沙漠"假设的传统悲观认知,证明这一看似平凡的假设实际上对理论物理有深刻影响:它不仅预言了标准模型的AdS₄真空结构及其全息对偶,还暗示了大尺度额外维度的必然存在。这为理解标准模型的深层结构、探索量子引力与规范理论的联系提供了新视角,将"噩梦场景"转化为富有预言性的理论框架。

Abstract: The absence of new physics beyond neutrino masses between the weak scale and the Planck scale is referred to as the great desert. It is often seen as a nightmare scenario for particle physics. Here we argue, by contrast, that the great desert assumption in fact has dramatic implications: it predicts that the Standard Model has a semi-classical AdS$_4$ vacuum with CFT$_3$ dual and, under reasonable assumptions, must be accompanied by at least one extra dimension significantly larger than Planck length. We also present one plausible candidate CFT$_3$ to be ``the" dual to the Standard Model.

</details>


### [116] [Persistent Spontaneous Time-Reversal Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16162)
*Michael Smolkin,Lev Yung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在2+1维局域、幺正、紫外完备的量子场论中实现了自发宇称和时间反演对称性破缺且无热恢复的解析方案,通过Yukawa形变诱导重整化群流,在有限临界温度以上发生逆热相变并动力学生成费米子质量,破缺相持续到任意高温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热相变理论中,对称性破缺通常在高温下恢复。本文旨在构造一个解析可控的量子场论模型,实现反常的逆热相变现象:零温时保持对称性,而高温时自发破缺宇称和时间反演对称性且不发生热恢复,这对理解量子场论中的非平凡相结构和临界现象具有重要意义。

Method: 理论构造基于临界双圆锥矢量模型与自由无质量Dirac费米子乘积的相关Yukawa形变。通过分析该形变诱导的重整化群流动,确定红外端点为临界矢量模型与共形Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型的解耦乘积。利用有限温度场论方法研究相变行为,计算有效势和序参量以确定相结构。

Result: 在零温度下,真空态保持宇称和时间反演对称性。当温度升高至有限临界温度时,系统发生逆热相变。在临界温度之上,动力学生成在宇称和时间反演变换下为奇的费米子质量,对称性自发破缺。破缺相在任意高温下持续存在,不发生通常预期的对称性热恢复。

Conclusion: 本文在有限场数的局域、幺正、紫外完备的2+1维量子场论框架下,解析地实现了无热恢复的自发宇称和时间反演对称性破缺。该结果展示了量子场论中可能存在的反常热力学行为,为研究强耦合系统的相变、拓扑场论以及凝聚态物理中的类似现象提供了理论范例和技术工具。

Abstract: We provide an analytic realization of spontaneous parity and time-reversal breaking without thermal restoration in a local, unitary, ultraviolet-complete quantum field theory in $2+1$ dimensions with finitely many fields. The theory is defined by a relevant Yukawa deformation of a product of the critical biconical vector model and free massless Dirac fermions. This deformation induces a renormalization group flow whose infrared endpoint is a decoupled product of the critical vector model and the conformal Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model. At zero temperature, the vacuum preserves both symmetries, whereas upon heating, the theory undergoes an inverse thermal transition at a finite critical temperature. Above this temperature, a fermion mass, odd under parity and time reversal, is dynamically generated, and the broken phase persists to arbitrarily high temperatures.

</details>


### [117] [Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions for non-hydrodynamic poles and branch cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16164)
*Andrea Amoretti,Matteo Anselmi,Daniel K. Brattan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了热关联函数在复频率平面上的非流体动力学极点和分支割线结构，通过构造高斯Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，将分支割线表示为弛豫极点连续谱，并用弛豫时间密度的Stieltjes变换描述响应函数，揭示了动力学KMS对称性、正定性和稳定性对谱结构的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流体动力学描述存在局限性，热关联函数在复频率解析延拓后会出现非流体动力学的极点和分支割线结构，这些结构对流体动力学理论形成重要约束。需要建立一个系统的理论框架来理解这些复杂的解析结构，特别是分支割线的起源、性质及其与物理约束条件的关系，从而深化对热平衡系统非平衡动力学行为的理解。

Method: 构造高斯型Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，将分支割线表示为弛豫极点的连续谱；采用弛豫时间密度的Stieltjes变换来描述响应函数；利用动力学KMS对称性、正定性和稳定性条件约束弛豫谱结构；分析端点行为以控制局部分支结构和近边缘极点的标度性质；研究均匀分布和幂律分布弛豫时间密度作为示例；在动力学理论框架下采用能量依赖的弛豫时间近似，在零动量极限下提供分支割线的微观实现。

Result: 成功构造了能够产生分支割线结构的有效作用量理论框架，其中分支割线源于弛豫极点的连续谱；通过示例展示了两种典型行为：均匀密度分布表现出超越所有阶的极点-端点分离现象，幂律密度分布则展示了极点-极点终止与极点-分支点终止之间的连续插值；证明了动力学理论中能量依赖的弛豫时间近似可以在零动量情况下微观实现这些分支割线结构；建立了端点行为与近边缘极点标度律之间的联系。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个系统的理论框架，通过弛豫极点连续谱和Stieltjes变换成功描述了热关联函数中的分支割线结构，揭示了物理约束条件（KMS对称性、正定性、稳定性）对解析结构的深刻制约。不同的弛豫时间密度分布产生丰富的分支结构和极点行为，而动力学理论中的能量依赖弛豫时间近似为这些结构提供了微观物理基础。这一工作为理解非流体动力学效应及其对流体动力学理论的约束提供了新的理论工具和物理洞察。

Abstract: Retarded thermal correlators, analytically continued to complex frequency, exhibit non-hydrodynamic poles and branch cuts that constrain hydrodynamics. We construct Gaussian Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions in which branch cuts arise from continua of relaxation poles and response functions are governed by Stieltjes transforms of relaxation-time densities. Dynamical KMS symmetry, positivity and stability constrain the spectra, while endpoint behaviour controls the local branch structure and near-edge pole scaling. The illustrative examples of uniform and power-law densities exhibit respectively beyond-all-orders pole-endpoint separation and a continuous interpolation between pole-pole and pole-branch-point termination. Finally, we show that an energy-dependent relaxation-time approximation in kinetic theory provides a microscopic realization of our branch cuts at zero momentum.

</details>


### [118] [Instanton Interactions and the Axion Weak Gravity Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16165)
*Jarod Hattab,Eran Palti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了轴子弱引力猜想的排斥相互作用表述，通过考察瞬子的长程相互作用，建立了关于复欧几里得瞬子作用量中标量场依赖性的约束边界，该表述在标准紫外完备理论中蕴含常规的轴子弱引力猜想且通常更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱引力猜想可以用长程带电粒子自相互作用的排斥性来表述。作者希望采用类似的思路，通过考察瞬子(instanton)的长程相互作用来重新表述轴子弱引力猜想(Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture)，从而获得对该猜想更深入和更强的理解，特别是探讨无质量标量场在其中的核心作用。

Method: 作者采用瞬子相互作用分析方法。在瞬子分离的领头阶，这种长程相互作用纯粹由无质量标量场介导，而引力和规范相互作用是次领头的。基于此，作者建立了排斥相互作用形式的轴子弱引力猜想，该猜想对复欧几里得瞬子作用量的标量场依赖性形成约束边界。通过在标准紫外完备理论示例中进行验证，证明新表述与原有表述的关系。

Result: 研究表明，在标准的紫外完备例子中，新提出的排斥相互作用轴子弱引力猜想蕴含了通常的轴子弱引力猜想表述，并且一般情况下更强。由于该表述纯粹是关于无质量标量场的陈述，因此推导出一个重要结论：在没有无质量标量场的情况下，关于轴子衰变常数的通常约束可能会被修正。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将轴子弱引力猜想重新表述为基于瞬子长程排斥相互作用的形式，建立了对复欧几里得瞬子作用量中标量场依赖性的约束。这一新表述不仅在紫外完备理论中蕴含并强化了原有的轴子弱引力猜想，而且揭示了无质量标量场在该猜想中的关键地位，指出在无质量标量场缺失时轴子衰变常数的约束边界可能需要修正，为理解量子引力中的轴子物理提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The Weak Gravity Conjecture admits a formulation in terms of long-range charged particle self-interactions being repulsive. We adopt a similar approach to the Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture by considering long-range instanton interactions. At leading order, in the instanton separation, such interactions are mediated purely by massless scalar fields. Gravitational and gauge interactions are sub-leading. We formulate a repulsive interaction Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, which forms a bound on the scalar field dependence of the complex Euclidean instanton action. We show that in standard ultraviolet complete examples, this bound implies the usual formulation of the Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, while generally being stronger. Because this formulation is purely a statement about massless scalar fields, it implies that in the absence of massless scalars the usual bound on the axion decay constant could be modified.

</details>


### [119] [A $dS_4$ maximum in M-theory from Casimir energies on Riemann-flat manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16169)
*Miquel Aparici,Bruno Valeixo Bento,Miguel Montero*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文描述了通过M理论在黎曼平坦流形$T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$上紧化构造的首个完全显式的四维自顶向下de Sitter鞍点解，该解具有受控的真空能量和质量层级，并在高阶修正下保持稳定，为在黎曼平坦景观中寻找更高质量的de Sitter解提供了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦论/M理论框架中构造稳定的四维de Sitter时空是理论物理的重要挑战。此前缺乏完全显式的、自顶向下构造的四维de Sitter极大值解。本研究旨在通过M理论紧化在黎曼平坦流形上，寻找这样一个受控的de Sitter鞍点解，以推进我们对de Sitter时空在弦论景观中存在性的理解。

Method: 采用M理论在七维黎曼平坦流形$T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$上进行紧化的方法。通过自顶向下的途径构造完全显式的四维解，确保解在高圈和高导数修正下保持可控性。系统地分析了解的稳定性、真空能量、以及Kaluza-Klein质量标度与Hubble常数之间的层级关系。

Result: 成功构造出首个四维自顶向下de Sitter极大值解。该解具有真空能量$5.02\times 10^{-10}M_\text{Pl}^4$，质量层级比$m_{KK}/H\sim 10^{2}$，并在高阶修正下保持受控。尽管这是四维de Sitter极大值的首个范例，但该解在现象学上尚不可行。研究还识别出在黎曼平坦景观中搜索更高质量de Sitter鞍点的若干有前景的方向。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了M理论中首个完全显式的四维de Sitter鞍点解，证明了在黎曼平坦流形紧化框架下构造受控de Sitter解的可行性。虽然当前解尚不具备现象学可行性，但该工作为在黎曼平坦景观中寻找更符合观测的de Sitter解开辟了新的研究方向，对理解弦论景观中的de Sitter时空具有重要的理论意义。

Abstract: We describe a fully explicit, four-dimensional, top-down de Sitter saddle, constructed via compactification of M-theory on the Riemann-flat manifold $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$. The solution has a vacuum energy of $5.02\cdot 10^{-10}\,M_\text{Pl}^4$, a hierarchy $m_{KK}/H\sim 10^{2}$, and is under control with respect to higher-loop and higher-derivative corrections. While the solution is the first example of a top-down dS maximum in four dimensions, it is far from being phenomenologically viable. The solution also suggests several promising directions in the search for higher-quality dS saddles in the Riemann-flat Landscape, which we describe.

</details>


### [120] [Zero- and Finite-Temperature Casimir Effect in a Fracton Gauge Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16263)
*Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由无迹标量荷分形规范场在两个平行反射边界之间的零温和有限温度Casimir相互作用，发现该二阶张量场的五个物理极化分为快慢两个分支，零温时真空相互作用按传播速度加权，有限温度时两分支获得不同热权重并产生分裂交叉行为，高温经典极限下五个受限极化的计数独立于传播速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶规范场（特别是秩-2分形规范场）的Casimir效应尚未被充分研究。与传统电磁场不同，这种场具有五个物理极化态和多分支色散关系，其真空涨落和热涨落行为需要系统的理论分析。研究其在平行边界间的Casimir相互作用可以揭示高阶规范场的多分支结构的光谱特征，以及从速度加权的量子涨落到经典模式计数的转变机制。

Method: 采用变分分析方法确定能约束所有五个物理极化的一致边值问题；将频谱分解为三个快传播模式和两个慢传播模式；在零温下计算真空相互作用时对不同模式按其传播速度加权；在有限温度下引入Matsubara形式，分析两个分支获得不同热权重的情况；推导精确的热相互作用表达式及其低温和高温极限；研究交叉标度对有效规范场速度的依赖关系。

Result: 频谱成功分离为三个快模式和两个慢模式；当快分支速度等于光速时，零温Casimir压强约为理想电磁值的2.21倍；有限温度下两分支产生分裂交叉；归一化自由能和压强遵循不同的交叉轮廓；高温经典极限下，由于Matsubara零模式独立计数五个受限极化（不依赖传播速度），相对于理想电磁场的比值趋近2.5；获得了交叉标度与有效规范场速度依赖关系的完整描述。

Conclusion: 高阶分形规范场的Casimir相互作用展现出独特的多分支特征：零温时受速度加权的量子涨落主导，有限温度引入分裂交叉行为，高温经典极限则由极化态数目决定而与速度无关。从速度加权量子涨落到经典模式计数的转变提供了高阶规范场多分支结构的直接光谱标志，揭示了秩-2规范场与传统电磁场在热力学行为上的本质差异。该研究为理解更复杂的高阶规范理论中的量子和热涨落效应提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the zero- and finite-temperature Casimir interaction of the free trace-full scalar-charge fracton gauge field between two parallel reflecting boundaries. A variational analysis determines a consistent boundary-value problem that confines all five physical polarizations of the rank-two field. The spectrum separates into three faster and two slower propagating modes. At zero temperature, the vacuum interaction weights the modes by their propagation speeds; when the fast-branch speed is set equal to the speed of light, the resulting Casimir pressure is approximately 2.21 times the ideal electromagnetic value. At finite temperature, the two branches acquire different thermal weights and generate a split crossover. The normalized free energy and pressure follow distinct crossover profiles but approach the same classical high-temperature ratio of 2.5 relative to ideal electromagnetism, because the Matsubara zero mode counts the five confined polarizations independently of their propagation speeds. We derive the exact thermal interaction, its low- and high-temperature limits, and the dependence of the crossover scale on the effective gauge-field velocity. The transition from velocity-weighted quantum fluctuations to classical mode counting provides a direct spectral signature of the multi-branch structure of the higher-rank gauge field.

</details>


### [121] [Edge observables in Maxwell theory on null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16400)
*Dušan Đorđević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维Maxwell理论在零边界上的边缘可观测量，采用适应零叶状结构的哈密顿形式。研究发现在闵可夫斯基时空、BTZ黑洞和德西特空间的零边界（包括渐近和有限距离边界）上，除了通常的U(1)电荷外，还存在与零边界内禀对称性相关的第二个边缘可观测量，两者形成中心扩展代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对规范理论边界可观测量的研究主要集中在渐近边界，而本文旨在系统研究三维Maxwell理论在不同类型零边界（null boundaries）上的边缘结构，包括渐近无穷远边界和有限距离边界（如黑洞视界），探索这些边界上的对称性结构和可观测量的普遍性质。

Method: 采用适应零叶状结构的哈密顿形式（Hamiltonian formulation adapted to null foliations）作为分析框架，分别考察三种重要背景时空：闵可夫斯基时空（null infinity）、BTZ黑洞（black hole horizon）和德西特空间（cosmological horizon），系统分析每种情况下零边界的边缘可观测量和对称性代数结构。

Result: 在所有三种背景时空中，均发现除了通常的U(1)电荷外，还存在与零边界内禀对称性相关的第二个边缘可观测量。这两个电荷具有良好定义的准局域性（quasilocal），它们形成一个中心扩展代数，在傅里叶模式下表现为两个独立的阿贝尔Kac-Moody代数，且具有相反的能级（opposite levels）。

Conclusion: 研究证明了相同的中心扩展边缘结构普遍出现在渐近和有限距离的零边界上，揭示了三维Maxwell理论在零边界上具有统一的代数结构特征。这一发现表明边缘对称性和可观测量的结构不依赖于边界是位于无穷远还是有限距离，具有普遍性和内在一致性。

Abstract: We study edge observables in three-dimensional Maxwell theory on null boundaries using a Hamiltonian formulation adapted to null foliations. We consider three backgrounds: Minkowski spacetime, the BTZ black hole, and de Sitter space, where the relevant boundaries are, respectively, null infinity, the black hole horizon, and the cosmological horizon. In all three cases we find, besides the usual $U(1)$ charge, a second edge observable associated with an intrinsic symmetry on the null boundary. The two charges are well defined quasilocally. They form a centrally extended algebra which, in Fourier modes, becomes two independent Abelian Kac-Moody algebras with opposite levels. Thus, the same centrally extended edge structure appears at both asymptotic and finite-distance null boundaries.

</details>


### [122] [Stringy Gauge Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16455)
*Gerardo Aldazabal,Eduardo Andrés,Lucía M. Cabrera,Martín Mayo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在杂化弦环面紧致化框架下，通过双周期坐标和非对易乘积构造了能够描述模空间特殊点处规范对称性增强-破缺效应的场论作用量，提出了保留扇区的场变换以建立作用量的潜在对称性，并将该构造与扩展切空间的规范化双场论（DFT）相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂化弦理论的环面紧致化中，模空间的特殊点会出现规范对称性增强现象，但缺乏能够统一描述这种增强-破缺效应的背景无关场论形式。现有的描述需要一个能够在模空间不同点自然过渡、同时编码非阿贝尔结构的场论作用量，以及相应的对称性变换和协变导数结构。

Method: 1) 利用三点相互作用和对偶对称性构造场论作用量；2) 引入双周期坐标（doubled periodic coordinates）和非对易乘积来编码非阿贝尔结构的背景无关形式；3) 通过广义模式展开使模空间依赖性显式化；4) 提出保留扇区的场变换，在模空间增强点展开时包含通常的非阿贝尔规范变换；5) 检验相应标量导数结构的协变性；6) 将构造与具有扩展切空间的规范化双场论（gauged DFT）相联系，通过广义Kaluza-Klein模式与额外广义标架方向的对应关系进行分析。

Result: 成功提出了保留扇区的场变换方案，这些变换构成了作用量潜在对称性的基础，并在模空间增强点的模式展开中恢复了标准的非阿贝尔规范变换。该构造虽然是完整场论描述的截断版本，但已能编码包含最多一个振子激发的无质量和有质量态的表示。通过与规范化双场论的关联，证明了在增强点处广义Kaluza-Klein模式的括号重现了增强的规范代数，而在远离增强点时，通过旋转Cartan基可恢复相同的代数结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个能够描述杂化弦环面紧致化中规范对称性增强-破缺效应的场论框架，通过双周期坐标和非对易乘积实现了背景无关的非阿贝尔结构编码。提出的场变换方案和与规范化双场论的联系为理解模空间几何与规范对称性的关系提供了新视角。尽管当前构造仍是截断版本，但它为发展完整的场论描述奠定了基础，并揭示了通过旋转Cartan基在模空间不同区域保持代数结构一致性的机制。

Abstract: A field theory action, able to incorporate stringy gauge symmetry enhancement-breaking effects, which occur at particular points of moduli space in a heterotic string toroidal compactification framework, was previously constructed from three-point interactions and duality symmetries. Via doubled periodic coordinates and a non-commutative product, it encodes the non-Abelian structure in a background-independent form. Moduli-space dependence becomes manifest upon performing a generalized mode expansion. Here we further elaborate on this construction. In particular, we propose field transformations for the retained sectors that make up the foundations of a potential symmetry of the action and that, when mode expanded at enhancement points in moduli space, contain the usual non-Abelian gauge transformations. We also examine the covariance of the corresponding scalar derivative structures. Although the proposal must be regarded as a truncated version of a full, presently unknown field-theory description, it already encodes representations of massless and massive states containing up to one oscillator excitation. Finally, we relate the construction to gauged DFT with an extended tangent space by associating generalized Kaluza-Klein modes considered here with additional generalized-frame directions. At enhancement, their brackets reproduce the enhanced gauge algebra; away from enhancement, the same algebraic structure is recovered by rotating the Cartan basis.

</details>


### [123] [Unitarity and the Forward Direction in Theories with Long-Range Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16896)
*Luke Lippstreu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了长程力理论中散射振幅的前向积分及其单位性约束问题。通过非相对论模型,作者展示了如何使用修正的分布结构和扭曲波微扰理论(DWPT)来消除标准技术中出现的红外尺度依赖性,从而导出精确且无红外发散的有效场论(EFT)耦合常数界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有长程力的理论中,标准方法对散射振幅进行前向方向积分时会产生依赖于任意红外尺度的模糊界限。这种红外尺度的依赖性使得从单位性约束中提取EFT耦合常数界限变得不明确,需要找到一种能够消除这种模糊性并导出物理上明确界限的方法。

Method: 作者采用两个核心要素来解决问题:(1)考虑长程振幅的修正分布结构;(2)使用扭曲波微扰理论(DWPT),该理论精确处理库仑动力学。通过这两个要素的结合,得到了具有明确定义的分波投影且无虚假红外发散的振幅。在将模型解释为具有紫外截断Λ_EFT的EFT框架下,导出了对任意紫外完备化有效的截断依赖界限,并在每阶取Λ_EFT→∞的极限。

Result: 通过DWPT方法导出的界限快速收敛到精确界限,在微扰展开的四阶达到0.002%的精度。研究还发现,DWPT展开的每一阶都重新求和了短程微扰理论中无穷多个费曼图(这些费曼图单独计算会涉及多重多对数函数和完全椭圆积分),并将其压缩为紧凑的表达式。在所计算的阶数中,甚至不需要积分运算。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了长程力理论中单位性界限的红外尺度依赖问题,证明了通过修正分布结构和DWPT可以在微扰的每一阶都消除红外发散,获得物理上明确的EFT耦合常数界限。DWPT不仅提供了更简洁的散射振幅表示,而且通过系统性的微扰展开可以高精度地逼近非微扰的精确结果,为处理长程相互作用的EFT分析提供了有效的技术工具。

Abstract: Integrating scattering amplitudes over the forward direction, and consequently the unitarity constraints one extracts from such integrals, can appear ambiguous in theories with long-range forces. Standard techniques for bounding EFT couplings then typically produce bounds that depend on an arbitrary infrared scale. We study a non-relativistic model in which the standard techniques produce such an ambiguous bound, but which is simple enough that the exact bound can also be derived non-perturbatively and shown to involve no infrared scale. We then show how to derive bounds perturbatively, with no infrared scale entering at any stage. This requires two ingredients: accounting for the modified distributional structure of long-range amplitudes, and using distorted-wave perturbation theory (DWPT), which treats the Coulomb dynamics exactly. Together they give amplitudes with well-defined partial-wave projections and no spurious infrared divergences. Interpreting the model as an EFT with an ultraviolet cutoff $Λ_{\rm EFT}$, we derive cutoff-dependent bounds valid for arbitrary UV completions. Taking $Λ_{\rm EFT}\to\infty$ at each order yields bounds that rapidly converge to the exact bound, reaching $0.002\%$ accuracy at fourth order in the expansion. Finally, we demonstrate that every order of the DWPT expansion resums infinitely many Feynman diagrams of short-range perturbation theory, which individually evaluate to multiple polylogarithms and complete elliptic integrals, into a compact expression. At the orders we compute, no integration is even required, suggesting that DWPT may offer a simpler representation of scattering amplitudes than standard perturbation theory.

</details>


### [124] [Positivity Bounds in $\mathcal N =1$ Supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16924)
*Jingxuan Bu,Jiayin Gu,Runqing Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将EFT的正定性约束bootstrap框架扩展到N=1超对称理论，研究了超对称Ward恒等式如何关联不同散射过程的Wilson系数，并明确展示了8维算符的正定性约束及其相互依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了确保低能有效场论(EFT)具有自洽的紫外完备性，必须满足局域性、幺正性、解析性和洛伦兹不变性等基本原则。在超对称理论中，超庞加莱代数将玻色和费米自由度联系在同一超多重态内，超对称Ward恒等式会对不同散射振幅施加线性约束。因此需要研究超对称框架下Wilson系数的正定性约束如何相互关联，以及这种约束对理论一致性和实验现象学的影响。

Method: 通过2→2前向弹性散射振幅的色散关系推导正定性约束；利用超对称Ward恒等式(SWIs)建立不同组分散射振幅之间的精确线性关系；聚焦于8维算符，分析标量、费米子和规范场的四次相互作用；通过SWIs展示不同2→2过程相关的Wilson系数如何相互约束，使其正定性边界相互依赖。

Result: 明确展示了N=1超对称理论中8维算符层面，涉及标量、费米子和规范场的四次相互作用的正定性约束；证明了这些正定性约束作为超对称Ward恒等式的直接结果是相互关联的；揭示了不同散射过程的Wilson系数通过SWIs产生相互依赖的正定性边界。

Conclusion: 成功将EFT的bootstrap框架推广到N=1超对称理论，建立了超对称Ward恒等式与Wilson系数正定性约束之间的明确联系。该框架的代数性质可自然扩展到圈级超振幅和高点过程，为超对称有效场论提供了统一的bootstrap视角，对理论自洽性检验和实验唯象学研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: For a low-energy Effective Field Theory (EFT) to admit a consistent ultraviolet (UV) completion, it must adhere to the fundamental principles of locality, unitarity, analyticity, and Lorentz invariance. This leads to positivity constraints on certain Wilson coefficients via dispersion relations of $2\rightarrow2$ forward elastic amplitudes, which carry significant implications for both theoretical consistency and experimental phenomenology. In this work, we extend this bootstrap framework to $\mathcal N = 1$ supersymmetric theories, where the super-Poincaré algebra relates the bosonic and fermionic degrees of freedom within a single supermultiplet. Supersymmetric Ward identities (SWIs) enforce exact linear relations among component scattering amplitudes. Consequently, the Wilson coefficients associated with different $2\rightarrow2$ processes are mutually constrained, rendering their respective positivity bounds dependent. Focusing on dimension-8 operators, we explicitly show the positivity bounds on quartic interactions involving scalars, fermions and gauge fields, and demonstrate how they are related as a direct consequence of the SWIs. Furthermore, we expect that the algebraic nature of this framework naturally extends to loop-level superamplitudes and higher-point processes, providing a unified bootstrap perspective on supersymmetric EFTs.

</details>


### [125] [Constraints on Kasner Exponents from Holography and Energy Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16980)
*Zi-Hao Li,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了平面AdS黑洞内部的Kasner标度行为能否作为全息对偶理论一致性的诊断工具，通过分析内部敏感的全息探针（如复杂度和熵）推导出Kasner指数的代数边界条件，并证明经典能量条件可以保证这些边界得到满足，从而建立了体能量条件与全息探针选择的内部几何之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自底向上全息理论中的核心问题是判断给定的体有效理论是否存在一致的全息对偶。本文旨在探索平面AdS黑洞内部近奇点的Kasner标度行为是否可以作为一种基于内部敏感全息探针的有效诊断工具，以此来判断全息对偶的一致性。

Method: 研究方法包括：（1）分析控制内部敏感观测量的度规组合，包括复杂度=体积（CV）、复杂度=作用量（CA）的体贡献、Hartman-Maldacena熵以及热a函数；（2）针对相关半经典区域（有限半径晚期CV和HM分支、有限CA复杂度、近奇点有限热a函数）推导Kasner指数(p_t, p_s)的代数边界；（3）检验经典能量条件（零能量条件和主导能量条件）作为充分判据，确保Kasner指数落在全息允许的区域内。

Result: 研究获得了以下结果：（1）推导出Kasner指数(p_t, p_s)必须满足的代数不等式边界，这些边界界定了Kasner空间中终端标度行为单独足以实现相应物理行为的区域；（2）证明了经典的零能量条件和主导能量条件提供了简单的充分判据，可以确保Kasner指数落在这些全息允许的区域内；（3）建立了经典体能量条件与全息探针所选择的内部几何之间的直接对应关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明Kasner标度行为可以作为自底向上全息理论中的一种互补诊断工具。经典能量条件与全息探针选择的内部几何之间存在直接联系，这为判断体有效理论是否具有一致全息对偶提供了新的判据。终端Kasner标度的代数约束可以用来检验全息对偶的一致性，而经典能量条件则提供了实现这些约束的简洁物理条件。

Abstract: A central question in bottom-up holography is whether a given bulk effective theory admits a consistent holographic dual. In this work, we explore whether the near-singularity Kasner scaling of planar AdS black hole interiors can serve as a useful diagnostic based on interior-sensitive holographic probes. By examining the metric combinations that control interior-sensitive observables, including Complexity=Volume (CV), the bulk contribution to Complexity=Action (CA), Hartman-Maldacena (HM) entropy, and the thermal $a$-function, we derive algebraic bounds on the Kasner exponents $(p_t,p_s)$ for the relevant semi-classical regimes: finite-radius late-time branches of CV and HM, finite CA complexity, and a finite near-singularity thermal $a$-function. The resulting inequalities delineate the corresponding regions of Kasner space in which the terminal scaling alone is sufficient to realize these behaviors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that classical energy conditions, specifically the null and dominant energy conditions, provide simple sufficient criteria ensuring that the Kasner exponents fall within these holographically allowed regions. These results establish a direct connection between classical bulk energy conditions and the interior geometry selected by holographic probes, and suggest that Kasner scaling can provide a complementary diagnostic in bottom-up holography.

</details>


### [126] [Carrollian Conformal Dynamics and Flat-Space Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17086)
*Simon Pekar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种度规-仿射形式的共形动力学框架，用于处理渐近平坦时空的共形边界上的Carrollian几何，并将其应用于四维Einstein引力理论，通过Penrose共形紧化将零无穷远处理为共形Carrollian流形，几何地实现了平坦空间全息中的辐射自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近平坦时空的共形边界是零类(null)的，因此具有Carrollian几何而非Lorentzian几何。这使得对称性、变分原理和演化方程之间的关系发生本质变化：与Lorentzian Levi-Civita联络不同，与共形结构相容的无挠Carrollian联络包含真正独立的数据。需要发展新的形式体系来追踪这些联络自由度，并理解其在引力理论中的物理意义。

Method: 发展了共形动力学的度规-仿射(metric-affine)形式，通过超动量(hypermomenta)追踪联络自由度。利用局域Carroll boost、旋转、Weyl变换和微分同胚导出协变演化方程组。将该框架应用于四维渐近平坦时空中的Einstein引力，采用Penrose共形紧化将零无穷远识别为共形Carrollian流形，其相容联络的未确定横向对称部分对应渐近Bondi剪切。在固定BMS(Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs)标架后，将Carrollian动量和超动量与Bondi数据对应。

Result: 一般的Carrollian演化方程成功重现了质量和角动量aspects的标准演化方程。该方法提供了引力演化方程的边界内禀导出方式，并给出了平坦空间全息中辐射自由度的几何实现。联络的未确定部分(横向对称部分)被识别为渐近Bondi剪切，这是该框架中的关键物理自由度。

Conclusion: 本文建立了处理渐近平坦时空共形边界上Carrollian几何的系统框架，通过度规-仿射形式和超动量概念成功追踪了联络自由度。该方法不仅从边界内禀的角度重新导出了引力演化方程，还为平坦空间全息提供了辐射自由度的几何解释，深化了对渐近对称性和引力辐射的理解。这为研究渐近平坦时空的全息对偶和边界动力学提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The conformal boundary of asymptotically flat spacetime is null and therefore carries a Carrollian rather than Lorentzian geometry. This changes the relation between symmetry, variational principles and evolution equations in an essential way: unlike a Lorentzian Levi-Civita connection, a torsion-free Carrollian connection compatible with the conformal structure contains genuine independent data. We develop a metric-affine formulation of conformal dynamics that keeps track of these connection degrees of freedom through hypermomenta. Local Carroll boosts, rotations, Weyl transformations and diffeomorphisms then imply a set of covariant evolution equations. We apply this framework to Einstein gravity in four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Penrose's conformal compactification identifies null infinity as a conformal Carrollian manifold; the undetermined transverse symmetric part of its compatible connection is the asymptotic Bondi shear. After fixing a Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs (BMS) frame and identifying the Carrollian momenta and hypermomenta with the Bondi data, the general Carrollian evolution equations reproduce the standard evolution equations for the mass and angular-momentum aspects. The result provides a boundary-intrinsic derivation of the gravitational evolution equations and a geometric realization of radiative degrees of freedom in flat-space holography. This is a proceedings contribution to the "Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary", held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

</details>


### [127] [Supersymmetric extension of the Hull--Lambert--Sen chiral boson on the 2-Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17161)
*Amine El Amri,Pietro Antonio Grassi*

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hull-Lambert-Sen手征玻色子模型的超对称扩展，利用Hull的双度规表述框架构建了该理论在二维环面上的显式形式，并分析了其与外部源的耦合。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hull-Lambert-Sen手征玻色子模型虽然在量子化处理上具有优势，但缺乏超对称性。双度规表述为手征玻色子的量子化提供了一个强大且易处理的框架，研究者试图探索该框架是否能够自然地推广到超对称情形，以丰富手征玻色子理论的结构并增强其理论完备性。

Method: 采用Hull发展的双度规表述方法作为理论框架，对原有的手征玻色子模型进行超对称扩展。在二维环面（two-torus）几何背景上显式构造超对称手征玻色子理论，包括引入超对称伙伴场、构建超对称作用量，并研究该理论与外部源的耦合机制。

Result: 成功构建了手征玻色子模型的超对称版本，证明了双度规表述确实允许自然的超对称推广。在二维环面上给出了该超对称理论的显式形式，并完成了与外部源耦合的分析，验证了理论的自洽性和可行性。

Conclusion: Hull的双度规表述框架能够自然地容纳超对称扩展，为手征玻色子理论提供了一个更完整的超对称版本。该工作不仅丰富了手征玻色子的理论结构，也为进一步研究超对称场论和弦理论中的手征自由度提供了新的技术工具和理论基础。

Abstract: We present a supersymmetric extension of the Hull-Lambert-Sen chiral boson model. The two-metric formulation developed by Hull provides a powerful and tractable framework for the quantization of chiral bosons, and we demonstrate that it admits a natural supersymmetric generalization. We construct the theory explicitly on the two-torus and analyze its coupling to external sources.

</details>


### [128] [Generalized complexity and dynamical response in holographic Vaidya spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17182)
*Mojtaba Shahbazi,Monireh Emami*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑Vaidya几何中的"复杂度=任意泛函"方案，使用Weyl平方泛函作为主要研究对象。通过数值分析和Fefferman-Graham展开，发现动力学复杂度与静态解的差异由边界应力张量及其导数控制，并在线性响应区域建立了广义全息复杂度与应力张量谱密度之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全息复杂度研究主要集中在静态几何上，而动力学时空（如Vaidya几何）中的复杂度演化行为尚未充分理解。特别是需要理解"复杂度=任意泛函"（Complexity=Anything）方案在动力学背景下的表现，以及复杂度与边界场论动力学响应之间的关系。这对于理解全息对偶中的非平衡过程和量子淬火动力学具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 采用Weyl平方泛函作为复杂度候选，对4维和5维Reissner-Nordström黑洞以及5维Gauss-Bonnet引力进行数值分析；2) 使用Fefferman-Graham（FG）展开方法系统研究渐近AdS时空中静态解与动力学Vaidya解的复杂度差异；3) 同时展开体积度规、极值嵌入、诱导度规、法向量和外曲率；4) 分析FG系数的逐阶贡献，识别首个非零贡献项；5) 在线性响应框架下，利用推迟关联函数和广义Kramers-Kronig关系将动力学响应表示为谱密度形式。

Result: 数值分析显示，动力学Vaidya几何中的复杂度同时依赖于径向坐标r和时间坐标v（"双重复杂度"），这与仅依赖r的静态解形成鲜明对比。通过FG展开发现，静态解与动力学解之间的前四个FG系数相互抵消，首个非零贡献出现在第五个系数，该贡献由边界应力张量及其导数控制。在Vaidya淬火过程中，导数项编码了边界态的时间依赖响应。在线性响应区域，这种响应由推迟应力张量关联函数支配，并可通过广义Kramers-Kronig关系用相应的谱密度表示。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了广义全息复杂度与边界场论动力学应力张量响应之间的明确联系。研究表明，动力学几何中的复杂度演化本质上编码了边界量子态的非平衡动力学信息，特别是在线性响应区域可以通过应力张量谱密度来刻画。这一结果深化了对全息复杂度物理意义的理解，为从复杂度角度研究强耦合系统的非平衡动力学提供了新的理论工具，并为"复杂度=任意泛函"方案在动力学背景下的适用性提供了支持。

Abstract: We investigate "Complexity=Anything" for smooth Vaidya geometries, using the Weyl squared functional as the most common candidate for our study. Our numerical analysis of candidates in 4- and 5-dimensional Reissner-Nordström (RN) and 5-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet (GB) shows evolution in both $r$ and $v$ ("doubled complexity"), unlike static solutions that depend only on $r$. We then study the difference between the complexity of static and dynamical solutions in the asymptotically AdS regime using a Fefferman-Graham expansion. By expanding the bulk metric, extremal embedding, induced metric, normal vector, and extrinsic curvature simultaneously, we show that the first four FG coefficients cancel between the two geometries. In contrast, the first nonvanishing contribution occurs at the fifth coefficient and is controlled by the boundary stress tensor and its derivatives. During the Vaidya quench, the derivative contribution encodes the time-dependent response of the boundary state. In linear response, this response is governed by the retarded stress-tensor correlator and, through generalized Kramers-Kronig relations, can be represented in terms of its spectral density. We thus identify a connection between generalized holographic complexity and the dynamical stress-tensor response, which in the linear-response regime can be represented in terms of the corresponding stress-tensor spectral density.

</details>


### [129] [Cohomological Aspects of Entanglement Entropy: From Information Theory to Noncommutative Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17195)
*Radoslav C. Rashkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了纠缠熵的上同调理论框架，将信息论、算子代数和非交换几何统一起来，通过Hochschild和循环上同调将熵的1-上闭链结构推广至量子情形，利用Tomita-Takesaki模理论提供动力学结构，为量子场论和全息中的纠缠提供严格数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论中熵具有1-上闭链结构，但如何将其推广到量子系统特别是缺乏局域密度矩阵的III型von Neumann代数情形，缺乏统一的数学框架。量子场论和全息理论中的纠缠熵需要超越传统约化密度矩阵方法的严格数学基础，现有方法无法充分处理无穷维量子系统的纠缠结构。

Method: 将纠缠复形嵌入到Connes循环双复形中，通过条件期望建立映射关系；将纠缠上同调定义为von Neumann代数到其子代数限制映射的核；利用Tomita-Takesaki模理论提供动力学结构；使用Connes-Radon-Nikodym上闭链作为编码相对纠缠的基本对象；通过Hochschild上同调和循环上同调将经典熵的上同调结构推广到量子情形。

Result: 成功建立了统一信息论、算子代数和非交换几何的纠缠熵上同调框架；证明了纠缠复形可嵌入Connes循环双复形；给出了纠缠上同调作为限制映射核的刻画；将Connes-Radon-Nikodym上闭链确立为编码相对纠缠的核心数学对象；该框架能够自然处理III型von Neumann代数的情形。

Conclusion: 本文提出的上同调框架为纠缠熵提供了严格的数学基础，突破了传统方法依赖局域密度矩阵的限制，特别适用于III型von Neumann代数。这一框架统一了多个数学分支的视角，为量子场论和全息理论中的纠缠问题提供了有力的数学工具，开辟了用非交换几何和算子代数方法研究量子纠缠的新途径。

Abstract: We develop a cohomological framework for entanglement entropy that unifies perspectives from information theory, operator algebras, and noncommutative geometry. Starting from the information-theoretic characterization of entropy as a 1-cocycle, we show how this structure generalizes to the quantum setting through Hochschild and cyclic cohomology. A central result is the embedding of an entanglement complex into the Connes cyclic bicomplex via a conditional expectation, identifying entanglement cohomology as the kernel of the restriction map from a von Neumann algebra to its subalgebra. The Tomita-Takesaki modular theory provides the dynamical structure, with the Connes-Radon-Nikodym cocycle serving as the fundamental object encoding relative entanglement. This framework naturally accommodates Type III von Neumann algebras, where no local density matrix exists, offering a rigorous foundation for entanglement in quantum field theory and holography.

</details>

Abstract: We study the decoupling of branes sourcing AdS flux vacua from the asymptotic bulk using scalar-wave absorption probabilities. In a one-modulus truncation, the result depends only on the dimension of the AdS vacuum and on the asymptotic steepness of the scalar potential. We find that for any steepness and for any dimension greater than two the absorption probability vanishes in the low-energy limit, showing that the branes decouple from the bulk. We illustrate the general analysis in several scale-separated AdS vacua. For DGKT we find $P_{\rm abs}\simω^{27/7}$ and an effective transverse dimension $d_{\rm eff}=20/7$. Interestingly, the scalar potential along the brane-induced moduli trajectory agrees with that obtained by compactifying a hypothetical $(4+13/7)$-dimensional gravity theory on a $13/7$-dimensional sphere threaded by flux. We also apply the analysis to scale-separated $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ examples and to the simplest one-modulus KKLT model.

</details>

Abstract: We identify a topological constraint on extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ spectra that is not implied by genus-one modular covariance of the three even spin structure partition functions. Thus the strict ansatz with no threshold primaries is excluded for this infinite family.

Abstract: The high-overtone quasinormal-mode spectrum encodes the near-singularity Kasner scaling of black-hole interiors. The reconstructed exponents satisfy the Kasner relation a posteriori, providing a nontrivial geometric consistency check. These fractional corrections therefore provide a direct spectral probe of the local geometry behind the horizon.
